The rolling clips (lab/recent) are the Lie Detector's, none of a jump attack.
- The user's play (watch 2026-10-24 17:05, 4.5 minutes on Tree Dungeon; D:/MapleHelper-scratch/jump-attack/user_jumps.py, ov-jump/user_up2.py, val_user.py): 138 jumps (31 a minute), 22% of the time in the air: in place 46 (69.5 up at the median), forward 38 (60 units at the median, 28-83), forward and down 25, forward and up 23 (57 up), in place up or down 6; in place again at the same spot every 0.98 s at the median. Of her 50 kills, 30 were of monsters 15+ above her feet; of the 17 after a jump, the attack key (put back from the EXP gain) came 314 ms after lift-off at the median (the top at 277). Against the pick: within 0.2 s of the attack's start, 23 of the 30 have a look from where the ground's search or the air's area or windows pick it, 20 by the old area (to 65) with the ground capped at 60 (within 0.35 s, 24 against 21; the other 6 no model has). Among them, from the ground, Green Mushrooms 69 above her 185-214 off, 74-76 above 223 off and 100 above 291 off: where the client's top (44) has the search pick them and the logs' lower top (21) wouldn't: suggestive, not decisive (review, 2026-10-25), as the ±0.2 s window put back from the kill misplaces where in a jump the attack came (the 6 no model has are all 61-133 above her with looks on the ground only), the first was airborne 0.09 s on, the third's only look in the window airborne as she lifted off, and the second has a single look. So the review kept the ground's cap at 60 above her (`UP_BY_SEARCH` off) until `probe run top-60`; asked, the user chose to turn it on now (2026-10-25), on these kills and the client's top. The shot logs' fit of the bands wasn't run again (the ground's model is master's). From a jump, one 58 over her feet 229 off: in its band's window (74 there), over the area's top.
- By the engine (the world, every phase of the game's steps; D:/MapleHelper-scratch/ov-jump/carry.py), a jump in place that turns her: the tap first and the jump 80 ms after it carried her 32.4 units (11.6-53.4), 120 ms 18.5, 160 ms 8.3, 205 ms 5.3 (1.8-7.0); the tap in the air 60 ms after the jump key 3.8 (2.7-4.3).

Of the jumps chosen at monsters above, in place 1.7 a minute grinding (0.3 on the hunt loop), forward 0.3 (0.2), back 0.07 (0.03); grinding's in place (16 seeds) 95 hits and 57 kills of 273, 60 missed (the world's noise: 27.5% of stars), 54 picked nothing (the monster elsewhere by the pick), 7 stopped. The hunt loop's loss is the rest's stall's, not the attack's (Found offline: a rest that a touch stands her up from waits out its 120 s): it stalled 71 s a run against 42 at the base. With the rest giving up once two looks show her HP not rising (an experiment, D:/MapleHelper-scratch/ov-jump-fix/exp3.py, not the stack's code), 236.1 -> 230.4 (+0.1% seed by seed, SE 4.3%, 9 of 16 better), HP lost 0.98 -> 0.82 bars, hits on trips 2.80 -> 2.01, 1 s stuck a run either side. Against the branch before the review's fixes (f2fdf3d, the same seeds, the stall in): 208.0 there, +5.7% on the base (SE 8.3%, 8 of 16); the fixes' cost is the ground's cap at 60 above her (`UP_BY_SEARCH`): with it off, 209.5 (+5.8% on the base, SE 8.7%, 10 of 16; +21.8% on the branch with it on, SE 10.3%, 12 of 16), in a world whose windows top out at the client's 44. In a world whose first window tops out at the logs' 21 instead (the area from a jump too; an experiment, exp4.py), the cap costs nothing: 198.1 with it, 191.1 without (+4.2% seed by seed, SE 9.4%, 10 of 16), and without it 34% of her throws from the ground hit nothing against 27% (stars at monsters above that the search doesn't take). So the cap gives up about a fifth of the hunt loop's EXP if the top is the client's 44, and saves only wasted throws if it's 21: `probe run top-60` decides it, and `UP_BY_SEARCH` is the switch (on since, by the user's choice: below). The harness's seed-to-seed chaos is large: before the review, the air turn alone came out -22.7% on seeds 1-8 (1 of 8 better) and +16.9% on 9-16 (6 of 8), so 8 seeds can't tell 20%. Grinding's seed 9 at the review (155 -> 37) was a rest trip that ended hanging on the rope at -8 at 180 s (Found offline).

The gain is in a world whose windows top out at the client's 44; with the logs' 21 (exp4.py, above) her throws from the ground hit nothing 34% of the time against 27% capped, for about the same EXP (191.1 against 198.1): what `probe run top-60` would still settle. Re-pinned, each docstring saying why: `loop_tree_23` on seed 2 (6 of seeds 1-16 meet it), `loop_tree_ore_waits` on 1 (4 of 16), `loop_tree_lie_detector` on 4 (6 of 16 onto the rope at 146 within 10 s, 12 on a safe place at 10 s); `tests/test_retreat.py`'s sweep 28 of 29 with the feed, 25 of 32 without, 30 of 32 shooting 23 from 24. Tree Dungeon's goldens whose hunts throw re-recorded (13: the loop_, feed_loop_ and tour_ ones but `loop_tree_lie_detector_28` and `tour_tree_rest`, which match, as the navigator's 7 do).

**Not done.** The navigator's own legs don't attack (a jump or jump_up move of a hunt-mode trip with a target in reach: the attack's lock, 730 ms, holds the chain's next keys, so only a trip's last leg could, and flow's chains don't know targets); a trip's jump forward is the stance's, between legs, in passing. Where she stands (`attack/spots.py`) still goes by the ground's reach. Unmeasured in game: an attack keyed at the jump key itself (the query starts at 40, measured: snail_jt40a and b rose 78.5), the air turn's 3.8 units (the engine's), the area's top over 18 (44 here) and the first window's (44, or the logs' 21: `probe run top-60`, which would tell whether `UP_BY_SEARCH`, on by the user's choice, has her throw from the ground at monsters above her that the star doesn't take), a jump's line over a step of her own group (`probe run step-up --style jump_throw`), and jump throws' hits at monsters above (the live check).

**The live check** (supervised: attack live tests wait for the user's go, 2026-10-24). The user starts the player (`python -m gamelab player --elevate`, clicking UAC), then `python -m gamelab tour --loop --map 10002073 --minutes 10` (the feed and the attack choice by default), and again with `--grind`. In its log, "jump-throwing right at 95 ms, above (...)" is the stance, and "    jump (above): a jump in place throwing right, the attack 95 ms in (95-400), 34 up: ..." each one carried out ('passing': a jump forward on her way; 'reach': one out of reach otherwise); the attack choice's line after it names the target above, not "nothing seen in its way". After, `python -m gamelab.analysis.jumps` on the run's telemetry: how often each fired, and the kills and hits seen after. Watch for a jump that lands off her platform or up on the one above (a short kick), a jump forward into a monster or past where she was going, a jump in place right under a monster above (the stance no longer takes one: her body would meet it), and stars at monsters above that hit nothing (by the model, up close only with the attack near the top: "the attack 2xx ms in"). From the ground, with `UP_BY_SEARCH` on: throws at monsters more than 60 above her that hit nothing (a pig 100 above her 271-357 off: picked there by the client's top, 44, not by the logs' 21) point to the lower top, what `probe run top-60` settles.

## Her throws at the dead, her MP, her damage and her air turns (from 2026-10-26)

The supervised hunt of 2026-10-25 23:43 (`tour --loop`, 340 EXP a minute; telemetry 20261025-234327-tour.jsonl, the perception service's 20261025-210747-perceive.jsonl) had her pet drink 25 MP potions in 11 minutes. Four lenses on it, each re-checked (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/analysis-1025/checked.txt), found where the MP went; branch work/hunt-a acts on the MP side (the scripts behind the numbers below are in D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/scripts).

- **No throws at the dying** (`ledger.dying`, `DIE_S`, `DYING_DX`, `GAIN_AFTER_KEY`; `Attacks.target`, `key`; `fight._targets`, `_shoot_at`; `Hunt._book`). 18 casts of Lucky Seven and 4 regular throws went at a monster whose kill had just been placed (about a tenth of the potions), 29 throws in all after the hunter had the kill. Three causes: a kill's gain shows 0.15-0.46 s after the key of the throw that made it, the server's, before the star leaves, so its monster is still drawn, dying, and the rule "one no longer seen" put 15 of 72 kills (10 piles) on a monster already hidden or gone; the dying monster stayed drawn 0.6-1.3 s, 14-25 units from where its kill was placed, and was taken from the path again, whole (a dying monster's bar reads None), with a new mark that the hidden fallback then followed for 1.8 s without the DEAD_LIKELY check; and the hunt loop made a new FightState at every new place or objective, which a kill's pile is, losing `dying`. Now a gain 0.1-0.6 s after a throw's key is that throw's kill, the first of its kind in its path, seen or not (`dead_one`, `Ledger._place`, with the gain's time: `Hunter.gains_told`); of the monsters of a placed kill's kind on its platform, the one nearest it, within 40 units, is the one dying there while its kind's death animation plays from the look that placed it (`MobBody.die_s`, the game data's die1 frames: 0.84 s for a Horny or Green Mushroom, 0.66 for a pig): no throw's target (the path worked out without it; the hidden fallback skips marks on it, and takes one not likely dead first, as the path does), no mark, not fought, out of the ledger's and her wounds' throw paths, and fight.kill waits for it to fade out; and the hunt loop's FightState keeps `dying` and its keyed throws across a new objective. At first every one within 40 units for 1.5 s: then, once the dead one was gone, a live one by where it died went unshot for most of a second, and grinding, finding nothing to shoot, went on (grinding, 8 seeds: 320 EXP a minute; the nearest only, 317; the animation and 0.15 s more, 351; the animation alone, 390). It's taken for the dying one by where it is alone: with the dead one unseen at the look (live, her looks missed 5-41% of the monsters within 125 units of her), a live one of its kind by where the kill was placed is, no target and out of the monsters about her (`fight._targets`' `visible`, `fight.dying_seen`) for up to its death animation. Kept among them instead (tried in the review of 2026-10-26: the stance's danger, its jumps' arcs, too close), the dead drawn there had her step and jump away from them, and on loop_rocky_bridge she fell out of the map (tour_hunt_basin, loop_rival, tour_hunt_other_spot and nav_fight_across_runs_out lost what they show, and test_retreat's sweep a seed): left out, as before. Replayed on the live hunts against the perception service's deaths and tracks (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/review-a-measure/scripts/replay_check.py), of the 147 casts it would have taken off a target, 103 were at one the service saw die, 10 at one it still tracked alive 1-2.5 s later, 34 not known; and 48 of them had another monster in the path, which the star takes past the dying one (the throw goes at that one: no MP saved). Within 30 units instead of 40, 85 dead and 8 alive (25: 67 and 7): the same share, fewer of the dead (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/review/replay_check_dx.py), so 40 stays.
- **A last look before the key** (`Actions.still`, `Hunter._still`, `Attacks.in_way`, `Feed.newest`, `pending_events`; `hunter.LAST_LOOK`). She decided each throw on a look 0.2-0.34 s after the key before, then waited out her lock blind: the look was a median 0.57 s old at the key, and 17 throws went out after a gain that showed before their key, 6 more before the hunter read it. Now once her lock is waited out, before any key of a throw or a jump throw, the feed's newest state (if of a frame after her keys' effect; nothing asked of the feed, nor waited for) and its events not yet drained: an EXP gain naming a kind in the throw's way (`aimed`, its target, its path; an unreadable one, any kind), as a copy of her meter tells them apart, drops the throw ("a kill came in before the key (horny-mushroom): the throw right is dropped, decided again", a telemetry event 'dropped'), and the next pass decides again. A jump back never is, and a probe's throw dropped so is none of the probe's (`probe._throw`: it scored one, for a star never thrown, until the review of 2026-10-26). On the way: `feed.fresh` compared a frame's time with a look's `after` strictly where WorldStep hands a frame what was asked within 1e-9 s, and a look waited out FEED_WAIT_S for nothing (simulator); now within 1e-9 s too.
- **Her stats and damage at level 25** (config.toml `[character]`, `maplehelper.config.Character`; `attack/choice.py` STAR, SKILL_STAR, SKILL_LEVEL, LUCKY_SEVEN_DAMAGE; `sim.world.LEVEL_25`). HP 774, MP 623 (her bars' numbers), level 25; her MP bar fell 2.345 px of 104 a cast (standard error 0.03) over 364 one-cast stretches of the hunts of 23:43 to 01:03, 14.05 MP: Lucky Seven's `mpCon` at 16 and 17 (13 at 14-15 would be 2.17 px, 15 at 18-19 2.50): 16. Off the monsters' bars with nobody within 200 units: the first bar 144 Horny Mushrooms showed after one cast, 231-346 dealt before defence (5-95%), 288 at the median, two stars of 96-192 to the unit (the most twice the least, as Lucky Seven's formula has it), which kills a Green Mushroom 91% of the time (live 45 of 53 Green kills opened with it took one cast); the regular star, 18 hits of 26-137, 80 on average: 20-140, as wide as the claw's star is by its formula at her first job's mastery. Lucky Seven's stars scale with its damage in the game data at another level (120% at 16). With them 'finish' fires from 49 HP left on a Green and 44 on a Horny (40 and 36 before), a regular star killing 76% of the time there; it rarely fires off a bar (none of 381 casts at a monster with its bar read was at 49 or less), mostly after a cast that likely killed ("the regular attack: green-mushroom ~27/233 HP if alive (48% dead); finishing it"). The simulator's Her on Tree Dungeon is LEVEL_25: those HP and MP, her stars drawn so with `spread`, 14 MP a cast (`Her.cast_mp`) and the 100-MP Blue Potions her pet drank then (`Her.mp_potion`: the first 20 drinks 95-100 MP each), where LEVEL_24 had 752, 601, 11 and 185.
- **Her MP under her pet's warning** (`Attacks._mp_read`, `_believed`, WARNING_STEPS, WARNING_S, MP_FIRST_S, MP_SHOWS_S; `Actions.throw`). Her MP bar read 0 for up to 0.6 s after each cast that took her under the pet's 5% warning (26 runs of 0 reads, each from 0.20-0.46 s after a cast's key, 24 ending in the pet's drink; no look ever read 4 or 5 px, the bar 6-9 px before each), while she still had 20-35 MP: 11 regular throws went out for 'MP 0'. A 0 read within 2 s of a read above 0 is now her MP as reckoned (the last read less her casts since) where that read, or the reckoning, is 9 px or less (from 7 px a cast's MP is still there, from 3 px not; from 10 px and two casts, 5.3: replayed, two of the live 0s came so), counted once a run of 0s in the attack summary ("her MP bar's 0 under her pet's warning not believed N times", apart from the regular attack's reasons; it counted looks, up to 6 a run, until the review of 2026-10-26). A cast's MP first showed 0.21-0.53 s after its key (it was believed from 68 ms on): a bar read off a frame shown at t is less the casts keyed after t - 0.21, and her reckoning stands where it's within the casts keyed from t - 0.55 to then shown or not; past that, the bar (a drink, her regen, a press that took no MP: believed less a cast at most). For a day a read inside 0.55 s of the last cast was believed only where it was lower: with a look 0.2-0.34 s after each key, nothing that raised her MP showed while she kept casting, her reckoning only went down, and she threw the regular attack for 'MP' with plenty of it (the review: replayed through the branch's own `Attacks` on the four live hunts at level 25, 91 'MP' throws, 45 of them with a cast's MP surely hers; master's rule, 85 and 32; in the harness 11.75 a run, every one with the MP). Her pet's drink shows about 0.3 s after the cast that brings it, and a drink shown between the look a throw was decided on and its key went unseen until the next look: the last look before the key reads her MP too (`Attacks.glance`, from `Hunter._still`). Replayed as built (the glance off the service's newest frame before each key: D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/review/mp_replay_glance.py), 39 'MP' throws, none with a cast's MP surely hers by the newest read (without the glance 48, 9 of them). In the harness none: her bar is drawn now at the server's time (`world.MP_SHOWS_MS`, 0.29 s after a cast's key, its pet's drink with it; drawn at the key, a look under 0.21 s after it showed a cast she took off as still to come, 2.4 such throws a run), and without the glance 1.25 a run. And `Actions.throw` asks its attack key before it tells the throw listeners, as `jump_throw` does: a ground throw's cast went off at the next throw, with that one's key time. Which frame the game draws under the warning isn't known (`perception.hud.mp_fill` finds no blue on its row: a warning flash?): one frame of a 0 read saved at a supervised run would tell.
- **The air turn** (`physics.AIR_TURN_LEAD_MS`, `AIR_TURN_ATTACK_MS`; `reach.turn_key_ms`, `TURN_KEY_MS`; `Actions.jump_throw`'s `turn_key`, `JB_THROW_MS`; `fight._turn_key`; `world.AIR_TURN_SAME_STEP`). 10 of the 28 jumps that turned her in the air threw the wrong way by her frames (36%; none of those 10 killed, 10 of the 18 that turned did), the attack 25 ms after the arrow; the ground throws that tapped first, 80 ms before the attack, turned on all 68 readable ones; both jump backs went the wrong way. Now the attack comes 60 ms after the arrow (120 ms after the jump key) where her air attack still picks the kind (a Horny or Green Mushroom, 51 tall, up to about 145 ms), else as late as it still does (a pig 105, a slime 110; a snail none, as before): a jump throw's turn in the air, a jump back's attack (its star now leaves as she lands, not just before: its target is picked as the attack starts, in the air), the turning shots her jumps' coverage tries (`reach.shots`: from each target's kind's own turn key, `turn_key_ms` of its height; for a day every kind's from 120 ms, and a pig on her platform had no turning shot until her way down, 430 ms in on a test map's floor, now one at 105 (a slime has none either way: at 110 its pick isn't sure 15 ms later, KEY_SLACK_MS); a plan's turning shot goes at its own key, `fight._turn_of`). The path a throw's kill is credited by (`FightState.aimed`, `fight.attack_gap`) is worked out where the attack goes: a jump back's at its turn key, where it was 85 ms (review, 2026-10-26). The simulator turned her as it saw the arrow, whatever came at that step (2 of 201 air turns went the wrong way in master's harness); now an arrow acted on at the same step as an attack's key turns her too late for it (25 ms apart one time in six, 60 never). How the game decides isn't known.
- **Kill timing** (`control.actions.DAMAGE_SHOWS_MS`; `sim.world._attack`, `_struck`; `Attacks.LANDS_S`, `SHOWS_S`; `analysis.jumps`). A kill's gain shows 0.15-0.46 s after the key (median 0.29-0.33, whatever the distance), a Horny Mushroom's bar first drops 0.21-0.55 s after a cast's key (median 0.40, 85 casts; 0.31 for 16 regular stars). The simulator applies a throw's damage the server's 0.29 s after the key (its HP and bar, its death and her EXP), and its flinch, push and damage numbers as the star lands (0.52 s), where all of it came at the release. Her reckoning has a hit shown on the bar by 0.6 s after its key (`LANDS_S`, 0.85) and maybe from 0.15 (`SHOWS_S`, 0.47). `python -m gamelab.analysis.jumps` credits a jump with a gain 0.1-0.85 s after its attack key, to the latest attack at that kind keyed so, and sees its hits by the HP bars of the perception service's own scenes (10 a second), from its records when a `*-perceive.jsonl` covers the run (`service_records`): on 23:43's six jump records, 'reach' nothing, 'above' a hit, nothing (at one dying already) and a hit (its bar 0.46 0.8 s on, in the service's scenes only: 'nothing seen' by the hunter's looks, a kill by master's tool, a ground throw's 1.4 s later), passing 2 kills, as that night's analysis checked them one by one (`tests/test_jump_reach.py`, with those records in tests/fixtures/jumps_2026-10-25). The shot log (`fight.LANDS_FROM_S`) and the probes (`probe.BEFORE_HIT_S`, their scenarios' world: `Realism.damage_ms`) still go by the release: to redo.
- **Lucky Seven presses that took no MP** (scripts/nomp.py, nomp2.py: single-cast stretches between settled bar reads, five hunts, 461 casts). 31 took none (7%), and nothing came of them (no kill, no bar lower, where 84 of 218 casts that took MP killed). They're ground throws: 26 of 220 (12%) against 2 of 233 jump throws (1%) and 3 of 8 jump backs. Among ground throws, more right after a move (a chain or walk ended under 0.3 s before: 11 of 57, 19%; with a tap first 21%), at a close target (under 120 units 22%, 200-300 5%), within 0.5 s of a hit (6 of 31: knocked); not at the lock's end (under 0.88 s after the previous key 10%, as on average; 1.0-1.2 s 0 of 14), not after the pet's drinks, nor her MP's level or the look's age. No cause is found offline: nothing is changed for it (waiting out a knock before a ground cast would cost more than the 0.85 s a press that does nothing loses).

**In the harness** (Tree Dungeon, preset crowd, `--world zero-walls`, `--attack choice`, `--costs measured`, 24 seeds of 10 minutes, `loop` and `loop --grind`; after the review of 2026-10-26, whose runs these are: D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/review/rv_var.py, table.py, from frozen copies of the source). "Master, new world" is master's stack (30f9d3e with the branch's first commit, the harness's counts) in the branch's world: her at level 25 with her stars, 14 MP a cast and 100-MP potions, the server's damage time, the late air turn, her MP bar drawn at the server's time (master's stack runs the same to the digit with that as without). The branch is measured on master 30f9d3e; rebased onto 4ac1b2b (the CPU work) it runs the same to the digit (seeds 1-8, both). The world's new damage alone takes master from 210 to 361 EXP a minute grinding and from 235 to 360 on the hunt loop (8 seeds): her kills come twice as fast with her real stars, so the branch is weighed against master in the new world, seed by seed.
