EXP a minute is level within the noise. Grinding: 0.0% seed by seed (se 4.1%, the median +0.6%, better on 12 of 24); leaving out the seeds stalled on either side, +0.5% (se 4.9%, 13 seeds). The hunt loop: 346.7 against 332.3 on average, -14.4 (se 21.7), seed by seed +2.8% (se 9.4%), the median -3.0%, better on 9; without the stalled seeds +3.3% (se 4.8%, 9 seeds). Every stalled seed (over 60 s with no kill or pick-up) is a rest that ran out its 120 s, and every such rest stalled one: a touch stood her up from the chair and her HP stayed where it was (`tour.Tour.rest` doesn't sit her down again; found in the review, left as it is). On the hunt loop 17 of the branch's 72 rests in 24 runs ran out so, against 11 of master's 91 (24% against 12%; grinding 10% both): most of its lower mean, and why its rests are touched more often isn't found. The branch's own changes show in MP: 4.8 and 6.0 MP less a kill (se 0.7 and 0.6; higher on 2 and 0 of 24 seeds), 0.6 and 0.9 MP potions a minute fewer (se 0.2 and 0.3), casts at a monster dead by then 55 and 67 fewer a run (45% and 51%), and no regular throw for 'MP' (the branch as first reviewed: 11.75 a run, every one with a cast's MP hers). The potions are relative: see below.

Her touches: on the whole 1.5 and 3.0 fewer a run that hurt (se 2.9 and 3.6), HP lost a minute level (+0.01 and -0.04 bars, se 0.04 and 0.05). On trips, grinding 2.0 fewer (se 2.0); the hunt loop 2.6 more (se 1.8, more on 12 of 24 seeds), 0.26 bars more HP lost on them (se 0.14, more on 15) and 16.5 s more on the move (se 6.6, more on 18): she travels more, at master's rate a second (0.20 touches a second on the move, 0.21 for master). The branch as first reviewed (e21f9b8, 16 seeds) had 4.2 more touches on trips (se 1.5) and 20 s more travelling. Counted rule by rule (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/review/diag_trips.py, its 8 seeds), the trips it set off on toward a pile were 107 a run, against 60 with the last look switched off and 68 for master in the new world; after a throw dropped at its last look the next pass set off on its way 14 times a run, nothing in reach and 10 monsters in view, where the throw at the dead would have held her there its 0.85 s: the lock's time a throw at the dead no longer takes goes on the move. Tried in the review, none better: a hold that long after every drop brought the trips back to 60, not their touches, and cost 18% of her EXP; the last look kept out of rule 4's shots in passing, 4.3 more touches on trips (se 1.9; 16 seeds, the dying then kept about her, before her bar's lag); the dying kept among the monsters about her, 1.2 (se 1.9, 24 seeds) and 5.7 s, but stepping and jumping from the dead (above). How safely she travels is part B's (Cutting her touches), and whether the freed time should go on the move the hunt loop's.

What the harness overstates. It decides the next throw sooner after a key than live, a median 0.25 s against 0.35 (61% of its stances before the world's 0.29 s gain, 28% of 1,446 live: D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/review-a-measure/scripts/rv_timing.py, live_timing.py), so it drops 6 throws a minute at the last look where the live hunts' replay drops 0.7, and master in the new world casts at the dead 11-14 times a minute against 2-4 live: what the last look saves is the replay's, not the harness's. (Its kills come about as fast as live's, 10-12 a minute against 10-11, not twice as fast as said before the review.) Its MP potions are 1.7-2 times live's (3.0-4.0 a minute against 1.6-2.3): the misses (Noise.miss 0.275, fitted with the old damage) and the presses that take no MP (live 7% of casts; the world takes it on every press) aren't refit, nor the HP potions (LEVEL_25's pet at 45%; part B's world has live's 70%) or the detector's misses near her (part B's `--world near-misses`); its decisions' timing isn't either.

The branch's changes one at a time, as first built (grinding, 8 seeds; scripts/ablate.py: each switched back alone on the branch, 390.3 EXP a minute, 37.4 MP a kill, 67 casts at the dead a run): with no monster taken for one dying 385.6 (-0.4% seed by seed, se 3.3%), 37.8 MP a kill, 83 casts at the dead; with no last look 384.7 (-0.7%, se 3.7%), 40.3, 89; with the air turn's attack 85 ms after the jump key 355.2 (-7.5%, se 5.0%, 2 of 8 better), 35.9, 65; with a kill put on a gone one first, as before, 348.1 (-8.8%, se 8.5%), 37.3, 63. The MP is the dying rule's and the last look's; the EXP effects are within a standard error or two of the noise on 8 seeds (the review's hunt-loop ablations as noisy), and the air turn's rests on the world's rule for an arrow and an attack at the same step (`world.AIR_TURN_SAME_STEP`, built for it: 25 ms apart fails one time in six, 60 ms never).

**Goldens:** after the review of 2026-10-26, the 13 that differ re-recorded again (every `loop_tree_*`, `tour_tree_run`: her MP inside a cast's show time and off the newest frame at the key, her bar drawn at the server's time, a jump back's path), each still showing what it's there for; `tour_tree_rest` and the 7 `nav_tree_*` the same. Before, the 14 Tree Dungeon traces with fights re-recorded (every `loop_tree_*`, `tour_tree_run`, `tour_tree_rest`: her stats, her damage, the server's damage time and the rest change each from its first throw or touch), the 7 `nav_tree_*` the same; other maps' left as they were (not checked: Tree Dungeon's only). Scenarios re-pinned with the reason in each docstring: `loop_tree_lie_detector` 2 -> 4, `loop_tree_lie_detector_28` 12 -> 1, `nav_fight_across` 4 -> 2, `hunt_kill_misses` 8 -> 3, `fight_step_wall` 8 -> 3, `loop_lull_loot` 2 -> 1, `tour_hunt_other_spot` 16 -> 1 (its other pig from 1280), `tour_run` 3 -> 1, `nav_fight_up_runs_out` 4 -> 15, `nav_fight_across_runs_out` 27 -> 9 (the two known failures: of seeds 1-16, 14 of each no longer run out of time), and the rope test's 3 -> 2; the probes' scenarios' world applies damage at the star's release (`scenarios._AT_RELEASE`), which their scorer is built on.

**Replayed on the live telemetry** (scripts/replay_live.py, read only: the hunts of 23:43, 00:11, 00:53 and 01:03 at level 25 with the attack choice, 58 minutes, 1659 casts; the gains as the perception service recorded them; each throw checked against the service's deaths and tracks by the review, D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/review-a-measure/scripts/replay_check.py). The dying rule takes 147 casts of Lucky Seven and 11 regular throws off a target the nearest a kill just placed, within its death animation: 103 of the 147 at one the service saw die, 10 at one it still tracked alive 1-2.5 s later, 34 not known; 48 of them with another monster in the path, which then takes the throw (no MP saved): up to about 100 casts, 1,390 MP, about 14 Blue Potions an hour (21, said before the review, counted every one). The last look drops 44 throws (41 casts, 0.7 a minute): 21 at one dead, 11 at one alive (the throw decided again), 9 not known. Her MP through `Attacks` as built (on_look, the glance before each key off the service's newest frame, her casts; D:/MapleHelper-scratch/hunt-ab/a/review/mp_replay_glance.py): 39 regular throws for 'MP', none with a cast's MP surely hers by the service's newest read, where the hunts threw 84 so; without the glance 48, 9 of them with it (49 of 84 "thrown Lucky Seven", said before the review, replayed the warning's rule alone: the branch's reckoning then, only lower inside a cast's show time, threw 91, 45 with it). The air turn's later attack and the damage model change what she throws at, not what was thrown, and aren't replayed.

**To check in game** (supervised: attack live tests wait for the user's go, 2026-10-24). `tour --loop --map 10002073 --minutes 10`, and with `--grind`, after the user starts the player:
- throws at the dead: atdead.py (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/analysis-1025/mp-attack-check/) on the run's telemetry and the service's records, against 22 in 11 minutes at 23:43; and where kills are placed (jumps-perception-skeptic/killplace.py): on the monster thrown at, no piles put on one already gone.
- the "a kill came in before the key (...): the throw ... is dropped, decided again" lines and the 'dropped' events: how many a minute (0.7 replayed), and that the throw decided again goes at a live one.
- her MP: the bar's px a cast (2.34 expected at 14 MP), MP potions a minute against 2.3 at 23:43, "her MP bar's 0 under her pet's warning not believed N times" after the attack summary (runs of 0s, not throws: 26 runs in 11 minutes at 23:43), no regular throws for 'MP' with a cast's MP left (mp_replay_glance.py on the run, with the service's records).
- her air turns: jumps-perception-skeptic/airturn2.py (which way her frames show her after a turn in the air, and her star), against 10 of 28 the wrong way; and a jump back's star the right way.
- 'finishing it' lines and what came of each (a regular star at a Green with 49 or less, a Horny with 44).
- a press of Lucky Seven that takes no MP: `save-recent` right after a run of unchanged MP, and the frames of a ground press after a walk.

**Not done.** The no-MP presses (above): measured, not fixed. The shot log and the probes still go by the star's release. The dying window is the game data's death animation from the placing look: a dying monster drawn 1.3 s after its gain (live) gets past it (26 confirmed-dead casts live 0.84-2.0 s after the placing look, against 5 at live ones: review-a-measure/scripts/late_check.py), and with the dead one unseen at the look a live one beside it is taken for it (no target, and out of the monsters about her, for up to its death animation: 10 of the 147 casts replayed; kept among them, the dead drawn there moved her, above). The last look reads EXP and her MP: a monster gone out of the path, or one come into it, still takes the throw decided before. In the simulator: her decisions' timing after a key (0.25 s, live 0.35), the misses and the free presses, the HP potions and the detector's misses near her, as above. And `tour.Tour.rest`, live as in the simulator, waits out its 120 s once a touch has stood her up from the chair, her HP coming back no faster than standing (6 to 13 of 24 seeds a policy stalled so in the review's runs, either side), found in the review and left as it is (not part A's: whether to sit again among the monsters that touched her, or go).

## Control

- **Actions** (`actions.py`): every key plan she sends, blocking until the player has run it. Throws come one per attack cooldown. Whenever she walks, she presses pick-up every 0.15 s; on a kill's drops she stops and presses it 4 times (a press takes one item, a pig drops several). No plan's first arrow goes down sooner than 0.8 s after her last attack's key (`ARROW_LOCK_MS`, in `hold_back`, which every plan goes through, the navigator's chains too, as for the jump and attack keys and the attack lock): an arrow pressed sooner (up to 0.62 s after it at least) doesn't move her until about 0.79 s after the key (In game, below; it was 0.6 s until arrows held back that long were seen to set her off 0.2 s late). Her own walks already waited out the attack lock (`wait_lock`); the mover's walks and the chains waited only for a jump in them.
- **One move at a time** (`mover.py`), closed loop, for what a chain can't predict, such as climbing down a rope, which needs her lined up on its top, and a jump off a rope (`rope_jump`: onto it and to about the height it jumps from, a look there by her dot's own height, a climb to it if that's off by more than 15 units, then the arrow and jump, up held for the rope it jumps onto, or the arrow let go just before she lands). Also gets her off a rope she's hanging on (climbing down and off the bottom of one that takes her onto no platform at its top: 959's, under 17's block). A walk-off starts 12 units in from the end at least (`mover.MIN_RUN`; closer, she backs off to 22 first), so she leaves it at full speed as the route has her: from 0.4 units in she left 31's end at 35 units/s, missed 34 and went through the basin's cliff (harness, tour seed 3).
- **A route** (`navigator.py`): one look, then plan, then chain as many moves as are safe and predictable into one key plan. Like a player, she doesn't go the same way every time: each trip weighs every move by a random 1 to 1.35, and moves start from varied spots. From platform 1 down to 34, 14 different routes came up in 40 trips, all within 1-2% of the quickest. Over a long route the weights average out, so ways more than a few percent apart never swap; with `--ways 3` a trip chooses among whole ways, and keeps to the one it took through its replans (Route diversity). Chains are capped at about 3.5 s, and the next one follows on without a stop where it can:
  - About 0.9 s before a chain's last landing she looks around (from where she starts, where she's going can be off screen). With an escort, what would stop her where the chain ends stops her there: the trip's `until`, or in hunt mode a monster in reach.
  - Once she's back on the ground for good, a look places her (standing: ground under her feet, not just her minimap dot, which put her on a platform she was still falling past), the next chain is planned from there (about 5 ms) and queued, and the player starts it the moment the running one ends. The running plan keeps her going for the ~0.3 s that takes: running on with the arrow held, short of the next move, or standing. That look skips the monster search (in game 110 ms, 131 at p90, against 205 with it): with it, none of 15 chains in a hunt loop followed on (seen too late to plan in time); without it, every one with room to run on did. Where there's no room for that (she lands right before her next jump, as all the way up the staircase from 34), she stops there instead; and when nothing follows after all, the rest of that run on is cut, so she stops where the chain was to end.
  - Nor does a pass plan from a look that shows her in the air (no ground under her feet on screen, `navigator.standing`: in the air her minimap dot alone puts her on the platform under her). It waits for her to land (`Mover.settle`) and looks again, and a caller's last look taken in mid-jump doesn't do for a trip's first pass. Planned from a look right after a jump throw, a run to a drop from 33 onto 35 went mostly in the air, and she dropped 30 units short, onto the basin by its cliff (harness, loop seed 30). In a 20-minute hunt loop a caller's look was in mid-jump at 4 of 66 trips.
  - Planned from where the running chain was predicted to end instead, errors built up: a landing 12.5 units off made the next running jump leave 12.5 units early, and landing late (still in the air, or sliding to a stop once her plan was over) the next chain's keys came too early and she fell off.
  - With an escort, chains keep clear of the monsters whose touch counts (`Escort.threat`, the escort's: those the danger model counts, `Hunter.danger.share`, a touch taking `DANGER_MIN`, 5% of her HP, or more at her level: on Tree Dungeon the mushrooms, on the first map none, where chains are planned blind as before, though whether the next follows on and the run on past a chain's end are judged by her box now: of 400 random first-map chains with 3 monsters seen, 117 follow-ons were refused against 68), planned as without an escort, and of no other (it fights them instead, at the stops); one follows on only where none of the monsters seen can get near it, and none stands in the way of its first move; the run on past a chain's end is checked against every one of them too. A chain stopped short of a leg such a monster could touch her on (`Navigator._meet`): she shoots it first if a star reaches it from where she stands (`Escort.clear_for`: a fight at it, one kill, never walking after it; `MAX_FIGHTS` shots in a row), else the move costs extra so a way around is taken, judged a chain's length on with the same monsters (`_around`: a way whose first move alone was clear sent her round to the other rope up to 18, whose top the same Green walked too, and back), else she hops it (one in the way of a run along her platform), else she waits for it to move off, but only while that can clear the way (`_worth_waiting`: its walking not known yet, it stands, or walking as seen it's clear of the move started up to 2.4 s later), else she goes on regardless, the chain planned blind as before (a backstop, `WAIT_LOOKS` or `NO_WAIT`; planned as if only that one weren't there, it stopped her short of the next, beside it), and so she does once her chains have stopped short of such monsters for 8 s without a move that got her where it was going (`MEET_S`: knocked back down to Tree Dungeon's floor each time she set off, a trip to rest ran out its 180 s there). On a trip to rest (`go(detour=True)`, `Tour.rest`) the way around comes before the shot. Her fights look at the band around her, so where the chain's legs go is searched again on the look's own frame when the band doesn't take it in (`trip_monsters`: from as high as a jump over the chain's highest platform, her body and the map's tallest monster reach, to as low as its lowest and the tallest monster under it, the look's own monsters kept too; and those her memory has there that the look couldn't have found, last seen off its view across within 2 s: `Navigator.remembered`, Her memory of the monsters). Live on Tree Dungeon on 2026-10-24, before this, 74 of 186 touches came on her trips (33% of the HP lost), most of them in the air, on a rope or after a landing, which nothing checked. In the simulator (the hunt loop on Tree Dungeon, crowd preset, `--world lucky-seven --world zero-walls`, 20 seeds of 20 minutes): touches that hurt on her trips 16.8 a minute of travel against 21.0, HP lost on them 1.49 bars a minute against 2.18, trips 4.5 s on average against 6.3, deaths 5 against 14, EXP a minute +43%. (With the review's fixes after that, the search under the chain and a walker turning back mid-leg, 8 seeds of 10 minutes ran the same on 7 seeds: from a trip's route to its end, waits and shots included, 14.2 touches that hurt a minute against 18.3 at base, 1.39 HP bars a minute against 2.01, EXP a minute 210.6 against 186.8; merged with positioning, 14.0, 1.30 and 202.3: Positioning, its last table.) Single trips past one Horny Mushroom (16 seeds each, transit): the jump from 24 onto 23 beside one hurt 0 times against 1.19 a trip, the drop from 22 onto one 0.12 against 1.06, the climb past one at 10's rope top 0.06 against 0.12; past a Green Mushroom walking both rope tops up to 18, 1.12 against 1.19 (wherever it heads it can be at either). What it doesn't reach: replayed along her real path with these boxes, the 48 hits on the way in the live hunts of 2026-10-24 had the monster that touched her in the look before the chain for 15, and in any look from 1.5 s before the chain to 0.3 s after it (as much as `trip_monsters` could search) for 22 at most; 26 came from monsters no look saw before the chain, 24 of them within 100 units of a spawn point (respawns: 11 on the jump from 24 onto 23). And a chain stopped short of one often goes on regardless in the end: in the simulator 167 of 512 chains (8 seeds of 10 minutes) came after a backstop, with 42% of the touches that hurt on trips. A respawn belief (the map's spawn points) where a chain lands and ends, and a way around priced by the touch's share of her HP, are next. Which kinds count is the danger model's (`Hunter.danger`, Positioning) since the two met, not a touch damage of its own; and the trips' tracks keep a monster on its platform (`MonsterTrack.reach`) where the danger model lets a kind that hops leave its own: no kind whose touch counts hops on her three maps (`test_danger.py` says so of each), and the navigator says it of a map where one does (`Navigator._threats`).
  - Where she stops, the look the next pass needs is taken once she has stopped sliding (0.35 s at most): it used to be two looks in a row there, about 0.4 s standing (0.6 s with the slide). A trip also starts from its caller's last look if no key plan was sent since (`go(scene=...)`): the hunt loop's decision goes straight to the first chain.
  - In game on 2026-10-20 (a 3-minute hunt loop): the stops between chains were 0.2-0.5 s, against about 0.4 s the day before, and 0.75-0.8 s with the slow look. Most chains on this map still end where she lands right before her next jump, with no room to look first, and stop there.
  - In the simulator, over 164 paired trips per case: without monsters, trips took 11% less time in transit and 15% less avoiding monsters, with misses from 20 to 9 and from 68 to 10 (the queue that followed on from predicted ends was most of those). With monsters the differences are within the noise: hunt mode 3% faster, avoiding 2% faster, transit 7% slower (one standard error), with as many misses and hits as before.
  - What it records (telemetry, for the mini-map and the coverage report): each route as planned, with where it was planned from (`from`; since 2026-10-21's evening), and the platforms its trip keeps off and the moves it costs extra for a monster in their way when there are any, and at a trip's first plan with `--ways` the ways it chose among (`ways`: each way's move, cost and chance) and which it took (`chose`); each chain as sent; and after each chain, or move made alone, a `moved` record: the moves made, where it was to end, where she was then, and whether she was hit on the way (before, a miss was only in the log, "ended on ... instead of"). Nothing is built without a recorder.

  Meanwhile it glances at her minimap dot and HP about 15 times a second: a hit, or her standing on a platform the running leg doesn't go to, cuts both plans short, and it replans from wherever she is. What the glances show is judged by how long it lasts, not by how many glances show it, so it means the same however often she's glanced at (each glance has its frame's time, `Senses.glanced_at`): she counts as standing where her dot has stood within 0.6 minimap px of where it stood for 0.109 s (`STILL_S`; for 63 ms it can sit still in the air, near the top of a jump or inside one minimap pixel at full speed), off the path for 0.109 s from the first glance off it cuts the plan (`OFF_PATH_S`; 0.046 s once one has her where the leg doesn't go, `OFF_LEG_S`), and after a plan `recover` takes 0.046 s of her dot standing for her stopped (`RECOVER_STILL_S`). Until 2026-10-21 these were 3, 3, 2 and 2 glances 63 ms apart (`STILL_GLANCES`, `OFF_PATH`): n of them are (n − 1) × 63 ms less a game frame, since a glance is the newest frame when it's taken; at that pace nothing changed (Continuous perception: Glances at the feed's rate). She never counts as standing while the plan has her in the air (`Flow.airborne`: from a jump's, hop's, walk-off's or drop's takeoff until a moment after its landing; a climb counts, since below the rope she missed it). Standing still where her run along a platform starts while the plan has her 30 units or more along it (`OFF_SPAN`, by `Span.x_at`) is off the path too: in game a chain that set off 0.2 s late jumped at its planned time from short of its takeoff and fell. A look that shows her still in the air once she has settled after a plan waits for her to land once more (`FALL_LOOKS`): knocked down the 12-unit gap beside platform 3 to the basin, she fell for 2.5 s, and the trip ended "lost: not on any platform" (simulator). Without an escort she avoids monsters: a leg a monster could touch her on (her box all along the way her feet go: `motion.bodies`) costs extra so a way around is taken, else she waits for it to move off. With an escort (hunting tours), she fights instead (see Behavior), and keeps clear of those whose touch counts (above). If her minimap dot disappears, for example under another program's tooltip, it moves the mouse inside the game to clear it.

## Behavior

- **The ledger and the drop store** (`ledger.py`): every kill her EXP gains tell of (Her EXP number) is placed on a monster of the kind its gain named (else left unplaced: `KIND_ANY`) that can have taken the attack of one of her throws in the last 2.5 s (the nearest the claw's stance reaches, else any monster in the search's first band, but none whose star terrain stops: with the first band's only monster up on 2 from 4, none; the likeliest first, and preferring one that has gone; where it was seen last, a throw's path being followed from look to look); with none, in the 2.5 s before the look before, as the gain came after that one: a fight's last star often lands after its last look, and the sweep after it takes no look for up to 7 s (in the simulator's scenarios 16 of 629 kills went unplaced that way). A placed kill's drops become an expected pile there, and looks that searched for loot confirm or forget piles, each keeping what the last look that saw it saw in it and what that's worth by the game data's prices (`looting.worth`); a pile no kill of hers explains is someone else's. Her wounds (`Wounds`): a monster in the path of a throw of hers whose HP bar shows up or changes in a look within 2.5 s of it is hers, followed look to look until a kill of hers is placed on it, it's out of sight 3 s, or 15 s have gone by since it was first hurt (chased too long); the fights and the hunt loop finish these before she moves on.
- **The hunt loop** (`hunt.py`, `tour --loop`): instead of the tour's stops, loot trips and fights on the way, one decision after every look, by the first of these rules that applies: close quarters; a valuable pile (a drop worth twice the map's bronze coin or more on its own, 15 s away at most: at once with nothing in reach, else only if it goes first: EXP before loot, below); back to the high ground; an urgent pile (about to vanish, close: ahead of shooting only if it goes first); shoot what's in reach; in passing (while travelling, 15 s per platform; with a step of 150 at most out from under one above her); finish (travelling to another platform: the monsters she has hurt, in reach or a walk of 150 along her platform away, 8 s per objective at most); a pile on her platform; a pile on the way (travelling: a stop at it 2 s at most more than the trip by the planner's times, 2.5 s away at most, 3 a trip); a better spot; lull loot (nothing left on the ground and her kills due back); wait (at the safest place along her platform that keeps the ground's spawn points in reach: Positioning, below); and, leaving, the piles left then (the most worth per second of travel first) and the next ground. She leaves a ground by the rate rule: her EXP rate there below 80% of the best other ground's (the trip there included), or nothing left and nothing due back (the fast exit; that ground is skipped for 2 minutes). Objectives (a spot, a pile) are suspended by higher rules, not ended, and dropped after 60 s, suspensions included. Visits are recorded with their length in `lab/grounds.json`. The numbers are guesses tuned in the simulator (`docs/hunt-modes.md`). The rate rule and the mini-map's 'hunt' record weigh every other ground's spot and the trip there after most looks: worked out afresh each time, that took 30-60 ms between a look and her next keys; with the spots kept and the trips' times from one search, in the simulator's hunt loop (preset `game`, 5 seeds of 10 minutes, 5,425 looks) the stack's own computing before a plan went out was 2.6 ms at the 99th percentile instead of 53.7 (at most 3.9 instead of 58.6), 5.0 s in all instead of 31.4 (see Where the time goes), and every run the same as before.

- **EXP before loot** (`hunt._loot_first`, `Looter.yield_to`, from 2026-10-24). The user, on the live grinding run of 20:36 ("While standing on platform 19 with monsters on platform 20, why the player doesn't attack?") and after it: "The hunt should be driven by maximizing the exp gain". No pile goes ahead of shooting what's in reach by a fixed priority any more. One that will still be there once she has killed what's in reach waits; one at risk by then goes first only if what waiting would likely lose of it, at `MESO_EXP` 0.5 EXP a meso (a guess putting EXP first: a valuable ore on Tree Dungeon, 66.8 and up, counts 33 EXP, under a Horny Mushroom's kill), is more than the EXP she'd make from what's in reach over the trip there and back. What's in reach is the wanted monsters a star hits from where she stands or on her platform, their EXP over the time to kill them all (`Attacks.kill_s`: what each has left as she reckons it, with the attack she'd throw: Lucky Seven's 12.5 EXP a second at a Horny Mushroom, the regular attack's 3.7). A pile is at risk (`_at_risk`) by its life (`DROP_LIFE` 90 s, the code's guess: 0 with `URGENT_MARGIN` 30 s or more of it left once she'd be there, 1 with none, in proportion between) and, at least `RIVAL_TAKES` 0.5 (a guess), by another player near it once it's no longer hers alone (`OWN_S` 15 s: the game gives a kill's drops to its killer for a while, the simulator's figure, not measured; a pile no kill of hers explains never is). A trip for a valuable pile is called off when a monster comes into reach that goes first, and so is picking a pile up (`Looter.yield_to`: the looter's first look and each of its mop-up looks); the pile stays known and is gone for again once nothing is in reach. On a trip for an urgent pile she shoots in passing as before. A trip she's on stops for a valuable or an urgent pile only where it goes first by the same reckoning (`_interrupt`, from review on 2026-10-25): before, one that waited could stop a trip "in position" on its first look, no move made, the next pass sending her off again, neither shooting nor moving once her time in passing there was spent. (In the simulator it showed with `--attack regular` (her Lucky Seven key bound, the bars read empty: Attack, above): on seeds 2, 4 and 5 of the crowd's 10 minutes the old check stopped a trip for a pile the pass then let wait 7, 20 and 43 times; with the regular attack as the map has her (the table below) and over `loop_tree_ore_waits` seeds 1-8, no look's answer changed.) Each decision that changes is logged with its numbers ("sapphire-ore on platform 22 at x -167 (worth 120, at risk 0%) against 4.8 EXP a second in reach for 9 s, 5.5 s there and back: shooting first") and sent as a telemetry event (`loot_first`). `hunt.EXP_FIRST = False` puts the old priorities back (the Tree goldens then match the traces before, byte for byte).
  - **Live, before** (the 14 tours of 2026-10-24 on Tree Dungeon, 1,474 looks with a Green or Horny Mushroom in reach on another platform than hers, by `reach.can_hit`, not one her EXP had just killed): she threw within 1.5 s on 1,290. Of the 184 others, 78 were looks while she picked up a pile she had gone to (for a pile on the way 26, lull loot 24, a valuable pile 23), 20 a valuable pile's decision or trip, 18 close quarters and 21 the shoot rule stepping away from one on her platform (`stance`'s step back: kept), 10 in passing, 8 travelling, 5 an urgent pile's trip.
  - **Replayed** through the new decision (each look where rule 6a or 6 set her off, her attack that run, each monster at its whole HP, the pile's worth and age from her logs): of 125 valuable-pile decisions, 73 had nothing in reach (still at once), and of the 52 with something in reach she'd have shot first on 50 (2 a pile by another player, 0.6 s away); of 32 urgent ones, 10 with something in reach, all 10 shooting first. The 78 looks while picking up would each have handed back to shooting.
  - **In the simulator** (Tree Dungeon, preset `crowd`, `--world zero-walls`, `--costs measured`, the regular attack, 8 seeds of 10 minutes, against the code before; without the feed, then with it):

    | | before | EXP before loot | before, feed | EXP before loot, feed |
    |---|---|---|---|---|
    | EXP a minute | 73.1 | 74.0 (+1.3%, standard error 8.1%, better on 3 of 8) | 75.0 | 70.6 (-5.8%, standard error 4.0%, better on 1 of 8) |
    | kills a minute | 2.23 | 2.27 | 2.25 | 2.12 |
    | HP lost a minute (bars) | 1.01 | 1.07 | 0.94 | 0.93 |
    | piles picked a run (share of her drops) | 18.5 (82%) | 18.4 (79%) | 20.0 (86%) | 16.8 (76%) |
    | deaths | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

    Run again after review's fixes (2026-10-25), both columns of EXP before loot came out the same, seed for seed. The crowd world drops no valuable item and few piles get urgent: 0-4 decisions a run (and a hand-back or so while picking up), and the runs part from there by the world's chaos (seeds 3 and 5 are the same runs both ways). Looks with a monster in reach on another platform not thrown at within 1.5 s: 117 of 2,063 against 121 of 2,062 (its own unshot looks are in passing, close quarters and travelling, not piles). Where the drops are valuable, `loop_tree_ore_waits` (every Green on 22 dropping a Sapphire Ore while she shoots 23 from 24): she shoots first and picks all 4 up, hurt once; with the old rule she went down among 22's Greens at once, hurt 15 times for the same EXP. Over seeds 1-8 of that world (4 minutes): 4,645 EXP against 4,306 (+7.9%, better on 5, worse on 1), 25 ores picked of 31 against 25 of 30, hurt 76 times against 98 (10.6 bars of HP against 12.7).
  - **Not done:** whether drops are owned for 15 s in this game, and how often another player takes a pile she leaves, aren't measured (the live telemetry has 'gone' piles, not who took them); the trip's EXP counts what's in reach now, not what comes into reach meanwhile.
  - **To check in game** (supervised, `tour --loop --minutes 10 --map 10002073` from 24 at 231): with a Horny Mushroom on 23 in reach and a pile seen elsewhere, a "...: shooting first" line and throws going on, then the pile picked up once 23 is empty; "something in reach first: the drops later" while she's on a pile and a monster walks into reach; a "...: the pile first" line only for a pile near its end or by another player. `unshot2.py` (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/integ3/attack) counts the run's looks with a monster in reach left unthrown at, by rule and phase.
- **Her pet** (`pet.py`): its fullness read once off her Character Info (a double-click on her body above her name tag; the window must be hers, its pet panel opened if collapsed, then Esc), then followed in time by the game data's hunger (1 point about every 75 s), a Pet Food when it falls under 60 (`FOLLOW_FEED_BELOW`), what was read and fed kept in `lab/pet.json`. The hunter tends it between her moves (read only with no monster near her), and the babysitter `pet --every 1` without a hunt. With the Lie Detector alarm up the babysitter feeds it by the fullness followed and reads nothing; the hunter's care stops with the hunt (On the Lie Detector).
  - *While the client isn't fit to read* (since 2026-10-26; `PetWait`): at 23:21 on 2026-10-25 the game was full screen at 3840x2160 for about a minute, and `pet --every 1` ended at once ("not calibrated at 3840x2160 yet"), the babysitter starting it again every 5 s (`D:/MapleHelper-scratch/winfix/pet_loop_2321.txt`). Now, with the client full screen, another size than its eyes', not calibrated at its size as it starts, or the game drawing off it (the perception service's newest state), it waits: says so once (`pet: waiting: the game is full screen (3840x2160); no food is sent until it's right again (its fullness is still followed)`), sends nothing, food included (the game may be at its login screen, where a key types into a field), looks at the window every 5 s (`WAIT_POLL_S`, no capture), and follows on once it's right (`pet: the game looks right again (20 s waited)`); the time waited counts for the fullness followed as the game window's (the pet out all along). It sends no Alt+Enter in general (it runs unattended beside the user's own play), with one bounded exception so the pet can't starve: once the fullness followed says it's due a food (`due_to_feed`: under 60, or never read and 2 h since a food), the fault has stood 5 s (`window_size.STEADY_S`), no Lie Detector alarm is up, none was tried in the last 30 minutes (`TRY_EVERY_S`, kept in `lab/pet.json` across the babysitter's restarts) nor 4 in the hour by any process, and the client's picture shows the game in play (the HUD's three buttons, `framecheck.hud_seen` on the perception service's picture at any size: 0.77 or more on the 69 good frames as clients of 3840x2160 to 1280x720 show them, the Lie Detector's notice over them included, 1.00 on the frame of 23:40, 0.52 at most on the desktop and the play area moved over the HUD, against `HUD_SEEN` 0.7; the border's 1-px rows don't survive the resampling: 41-92 dark and 178-212 white, against 40 and 200), it makes the one try the hunts make (`window_size.refit`, the Lie Detector looked for first), said in the log (`pet: the pet is due a food (its fullness about 59) and the game is in play (its HUD seen, 0.98): one try at Alt+Enter twice, full screen and back`), and why it didn't once (`pet: no Alt+Enter: the game isn't seen in play ...`). The try is counted (`window_try`, the hour's) as its first Alt+Enter goes, so a try cut short (the babysitter stops the pet as a hunt starts, the hold alone 8 s) is one too; a look that came to nothing (the game not in play, no picture, no Alt+Enter sent) is made again 30 s on, not at every poll. It waits too while another command's try holds the lock (`window --recover`, which the babysitter doesn't stop it for). Its eyes are made once the client is a size they're calibrated for; a client windowed later at a size calibrated but not its eyes' (eyes made at 1920x1080, the game back at its own 1366x768) ends it, for the babysitter to start it again with eyes for that size (`pet: the game's client is 1366x768 now, a size calibrated but not the eyes' (1920x1080): the pet ends ...`), where it waited for ever, unfed. The in-play check has met no login or character screen yet (none is saved).
- **Her retreat from the Lie Detector** (`retreat.py`): the hunt stopped by the alarm takes her to a safe place within 10 s, her moves alone (On the Lie Detector).
- **The hunter** (`hunter.py`) keeps count after every look, whoever took it: a kill counts only when her EXP number goes up (counting a vanished target as dead was wrong about half the time: hit monsters flinch and hide behind damage numbers, and other players kill monsters too): as many kills as its gain's size is, of the kinds it names, and none for a size no kill gives (see Her EXP number). It drinks an HP potion below `--potion-hp` and hands control back below `--low-hp`.
- **A fight** (`fight.py`, `clear`): one throw per attack cooldown, with a look during each. She never walks past a monster. A monster of any kind that comes close on her own platform is in her way and fought, snails included. Reaching a stop's kill count doesn't end a fight while monsters are still in reach. Nor, in any fight, while one she had hurt by then is in reach or a walk of 150 along her platform away (`fight.owed`, `finish_act`), and with nothing else in reach she closes in on it, in the escort's fights too, which don't walk after monsters otherwise. An EXP gain seen by a look the fight didn't take itself (a sweep's, before it ended at its kill count or in the sweep after it) or by the look it ended on (knocked off) is still told apart as a kill when it ends. On a tour she also shoots monsters worth hunting on any platform in reach. A star hits the first monster in its path, whatever its kind, so kills are matched against everything in the path, not just what she was aiming at: from the ground what the claw or the search can pick by each monster's body (`reach.ground_pick`: not a slime 60 below and 150 off, nor a pig 440 off), but not one whose star terrain stops, nor any behind one in the attack's order (the client doesn't refill its pick); from a jump, as high as she is when the attack starts (`reach.in_area`): monsters on the platform above count as far as a star reaches, a snail beside her doesn't. A monster just hit (its HP bar new or lower since it was last seen, or hidden under its damage numbers) counts as tall as it flinches (the game data's `hit1` frames, for 0.6 s): a pig 60 below her is then in the path from 111 units along, against 151 walking. No jump throw, jump back or step (stepping away, closing in) is keyed from a look a touch's knock may still be moving her in (a look within 0.45 s of one that saw her HP drop: `Hunter.knocked`, `fight.knocked_move`; the hunt loop's shooting too): it waits for the next look. Keyed from such a look, the jump key came in the air and was lost, or went in once she had landed and slid on, from where she no longer was (Fixed since); a step walked her on from where the knock had put her, once off the end of 22.
- **Hidden monsters:** a monster she was throwing at is still thrown at for 1.8 s after it drops out of sight (hit, it flinches under its damage numbers, and the detector loses it for 1-3 s). A kill is still seen fading out where it died as long as its death animation runs (the game data's die1 frames: a pig or an Orange Mushroom 0.66 s, a slime 0.84, a snail 1.26), and what's seen there is left alone that long from the look that saw her EXP go up (`fight.fade_s`; 1.2 s for every kind before, and for a kind the data lacks).
- **Waiting:** with nothing in reach she walks just close enough to the nearest monster. With monsters seen but out of reach, she waits up to 2 s from a safe platform (1 s on the hunting platform). With none seen at all, she moves on after 0.4 s.
- **Fighting on the way** (`FightOnTheWay`): before each move, a short burst at whatever a star reaches from where she stands, on any platform: up to 3 kills, never walking after them (but for one she has hurt, a walk of 150 at most: she finishes it), up to 5 bursts per platform. It also shoots any monster in the way of a move along a platform. It's turned off while she goes to rest.
- **Loot** (`looting.py`): each kill's drops are swept when the fight is over, if they fell on her platform (it looks again until she has landed: the fight often ended mid-jump). After the sweep she looks again and goes back for drops still on her platform, standing right on each one, for up to 3 looks. Drops elsewhere are remembered for the tour: her kills', and any seen on other platforms. A remembered spot is forgotten only when two looks 1.5 s apart, 3 s or more after the kill, both see it empty. A drop she has stood on twice that's still there is left alone for 90 s: for mesos that means someone else's drop; for an item it may mean her inventory is full, which the pickup log (OCR) says, or 3 such items in a row. With it full she leaves items and still picks up mesos.
- **A tour** (`tour.py`) sweeps every platform worth hunting. A platform's worth is the EXP a visit is expected to give: at first 30% of its spawns' EXP (game data), counted as 2 visits, then blended with what each visit really gave (`lab/grounds.json`). Monsters giving under a quarter of the map's best EXP aren't hunted, only shot when in her way (snails give 2, against 12-15). Platforms expected to give under 15% of the best one's are skipped: by the data, 34 and 35 spawn slimes, but 11 visits found only snails there. The order: nearest next by route time, judged with a random ±25%, then tidied to cut travel (2-opt); after each stop the rest of the sweep is planned again from wherever she ended up. At a stop she kills all she can reach from her spot; if some are left out of reach, she moves to another spot that reaches them rather than walking down into them, and only then collects the drops (nearest by route first; drops of monsters shot on the way are kept for the next collection, and none starts a trip after 180 s). She moves on once a whole-screen look sees no monster left. When HP is low, she rests on the chair at the nearest safe platform (on the way there she still drinks potions, but low HP doesn't stop the trip), and she ends standing by there.
- **Grinding** (`grind.py`, `patrol.py`, `tour --loop --grind`): the hunt loop with the stops of a loop over the map's spawn points for its grounds, the loop chosen by EXP a minute, piles she sees pulling her off it, each stop left as soon as staying gives less than going on, what comes into reach on the way shot in passing (see Grinding).

## Positioning: where she stands, where she waits, how near she lets a monster come (from 2026-10-24)

The user, watching her hunt Tree Dungeon live on 2026-10-24 (`tour --loop --skill`, Lucky Seven): she could "hit a lot of the monsters from a far (even from another platform)" but hit the ones near her "without any good evasion menuver" and took the Horny Mushrooms' touches; and waiting for respawns she picked "a spot between two horny mushroom respawn points" ("This rule should be generalized"). The day's telemetry (the two hunt loops after the HP misreads were fixed, 4.8 and 15.6 minutes on 23 alone: `lab/telemetry/20261024-135648-tour.jsonl`, `-140147-`; HP drops under 0.6 s apart one touch, 12% of her bar or more a Horny's):
- 38 of her 56 touches and 78% of the HP she lost were on 23 itself, 23 touches a Horny's; `spots.spots(23)` had 24 at 211 first (98% of 23 in reach), which `spot_ok` turned down for 24's one Green Mushroom, so she hunted 23 standing among its Hornies. When the first monster in a throw's line was a Horny, it was under 150 units off in 38-49% of her throws. A jump back (110 units of room, nobody within 160 behind her) almost never could with a Horny each side: 1 of 128 stances before KEEP_OFF, 1 of 332 with it.
- With 23 empty she went back to its middle (x -6, `spots.spot`'s fallback), 9.5 units from one Horny spawn point (-15) and 101 from the other (95), 38 times; with nothing on 23 she stood within 20 units of a spawn point 37% of the time, and respawns came onto her there twice (11% and 19% of her HP); 9 touches while waiting took 128% of her bar. Respawns on 23 came 1.7-57 s after her last kill there (about 17 s at the median), not on `hunt.RESPAWN`'s 8.
- KEEP_OFF (bc036b9: a Horny kept 170 off, she stepped away or jumped back) changed nothing: 2.50 against 2.82 touches a minute, 0.28 against 0.30 bars of HP.

**The model** (`attack/danger.py`): the danger of standing at a place (a platform, an x on it) is the share of her HP touches would take there in the next moments, summed over its sources, never a kind or a map by name:
- **What a touch takes:** a kind's touch damage (PADamage, game data, now read with the map: `MapModel.mob_damage`) by meowdb's formula at her level and DEF 75 (`touch_taken`, the simulator's own since: `sim.world.touch_damage`), over her HP (config.toml `[character] hp` and `level`, 752 and 24 now; the simulator's `Her` in a run). A kind under `DANGER_MIN` (5% of her HP) counts for nothing: on the first map nothing is over 2% (a pig 1.7% of her 642 HP at level 19), so every decision there is as it was. On Tree Dungeon a Horny Mushroom takes 16.1% of hers, a Green Mushroom 7.2%, a Slime 1.2% (nothing); on West Rocky Mountain II an Axe Stump 10.8% of her 730, a Dark Stump 4.3% (nothing).
- **Whether it can get to her:** a monster walks its own platform and never leaves it, but a kind that hops (a jump action in its Mob data: `MapModel.mob_hops`; on the first map pigs, slimes and Orange Mushrooms, on Tree Dungeon the Slimes, not the mushrooms) can come onto a platform whose end is within `HOP_ACROSS` (60) across and `HOP_UP` (60) up of its own, `HOP_CHANCE` (0.25, a guess) as likely, and from a platform over hers only by its end (it can't drop through its own: the review found one 100 straight above her counted as dropping onto her); no kind that hops counts on these maps. One walking a platform just under or over hers touches her where they overlap, its top into her or her 65 units (`HER_TALL`, her box the client's: `motion/bodies.py`, which her trips go by too) into it: on 24 by 23's right end (37 above it) 23's Hornies touch her up to x 208, not at 211.
- **How soon:** its walk to where its body meets hers (half its width and her 22) at its speed (game data: 100 units/s for all three), weighed exp(-t / `THREAT_S`): 1 for one on her already (a respawn on top of her), about a third `THREAT_S` (1.7 s, two of her throws) away. A Horny 100 units off counts 0.71, 200 off 0.39, 400 off 0.12.
- **Spawn points** (`MapModel.spawn_points`, each with its platform; live, the first sightings of monsters back on 23 came at the points' x again and again): planning, each as its monster at the point; with a look, of each kind's points on a platform as many as the look's monsters of that kind there don't account for, each as one that may come back at any moment.
- **Touch damage comes from the game data copy** on Tree Dungeon (its cached map predates it): when that can't be read, the map loads saying so on stderr, since every kind would then count for nothing and the positioning switch off unseen.

On 23 the danger is 0.28 at x -10 (on a Horny's spawn point, the other 105 off), 0.31 midway between them, 0.15 at -147 (132 and 242 off); on 24 at 211 0.067 (its Green 61 off; 23's Hornies can't get to her there), at 208 0.24 (they can). (With her body the simulator's 15 x 55, as the model was built and the harness tables below were run: 0.27, 0.30, 0.14, 0.065, and they could up to 201.)

**What it decides:**
- **Where she shoots a ground from** (`attack/spots.py`): a spot's score is the share of the ground in reach less `DANGER_WEIGHT` (1) times its danger. Besides the spots `spot_ok` takes (as before), one on any other platform whose score beats hunting on the ground itself (all of it in reach, less the danger of the ground's safest place) can be stood on; of those about as good as the best by score, the nearest. On a platform where monsters that count spawn, a spot keeps `GROUND_MARGIN` (50, as stance does with monsters about) from its ends: at 211 on 24 a touch of its Green knocked her off its left end in the simulator. With none, she hunts the ground standing on it from its safest place (`spots.on_ground`: on 23 x -147, not its middle; still its middle where nothing counts). Better spots (`spot_for`) go by monsters reached, then danger, then travel. On Tree Dungeon: 23 from 24 at 231 (95% of it, danger 0.07), 7 from 8 at 147 (100%, 0.18), 13 from 12 at 168, 15 from 16 at 89, 19 from 20 at -65, 12 from 11 at -72, 29 from 28 at 197 (97%) and 30 from 28 at 277 (no danger there: `MapModel.safe` called 28 unsafe because 30 runs under its left part); 6, 8, 9, 10, 20, 31 and 33 hunted standing on them, at their safest places. Working the spots out for every ground of the map takes 1.1 s at the hunt loop's first look (0.67 s for the first map's 16 before).
- **Which ground** (`hunt.GROUND_DANGER`, 2): a ground's EXP a second counts 1 - 2 x the danger where she'd stand to hunt it, 0 at least: 23 from 24 keeps 86%, 10 among its two Hornies 64%. Where nothing counts, all of it. With every ground's EXP counted whole, `loop_tree_run` went up the tower to 23, 19, 7 and 19 for 1,041 EXP, not the floor for 1,311: it checks she doesn't (19, 7, 10), and `test_danger.py` that of two grounds giving as much the one with the safe stand is taken.
- **How near a monster comes** (`attack/stance.py`, replacing KEEP_OFF): she lets a monster of her platform come as near as where its danger reaches `KEEP_DANGER` (8% of her HP, half a Horny's touch: `Danger.keep_off`, 169 units for a Horny, 162 with the simulator's body; a Green, an Axe Stump and every kind of the first map only to the claw's reach, as before); then, for a kind kept further off than the claw's reach, of a jump back (throwing back), a step away and a throw from where she is, whichever leaves her the least danger with the monsters around her and the spawn points they don't account for (`stance._escape`): a monster behind her makes the way back worse by its own danger rather than ruling it out, and no move whose way or landing meets a monster's body on her platform, of any kind, is an escape (`stance._into`, as `_in_the_way`: the review had a step end 30 from a Green Mushroom's middle); a step away, which doesn't throw, only when it's `ESCAPE_THROW` (0.03, a guess) safer than the jump back or the throw from where she is (by danger alone the step, 120 to the jump's 95, always won, and she backed away without throwing whenever the way back was clear); from where she is it's a throw from the ground, a jump throw only where that would be a claw swipe. The hunt loop's rule 1 and a trip's stop go by the same distance. In the simulator (the whole map, `crowd`, 20 seeds of 10 minutes) the escape's fix made 196.7 EXP a minute against 203.4, 1.18 bars of HP a minute against 1.17: within the runs' spread, and its monsters never chase her, so what a step costs isn't priced there.
- **Where she waits** (`hunt._wait_place`, rule 9): with monsters about or due back, she walks along her platform to the safest place (Danger.at with the look's monsters, `GROUND_MARGIN` from its ends) from which a star still reaches as many of the ground's spawn points as from where she is, if it's `WAIT_GAIN` (3% of her HP) safer and a step's worth (15 units) away: on 23 from -10 to -147. Never past a monster that counts on the way (`_in_the_way`: where its body would meet hers), and in steps of `WAIT_STEP` (100 units) with a look after each, since the walk is blind and a respawn changes where's safest. Where she shoots the ground from a spot off it, she waits there: the spot is already where its monsters can't get to her (waiting back at the spot when she's on the ground for a pile was tried, and lost more than it saved: 0.56 against 0.49 bars a minute on 23 alone in `crowd-slow`). Never where nothing counts (the first map: she waits where she is, as before), nor from a look a knock may still be moving her in. On 24 she waits at the spot (231), 41 from its Green Mushroom's spawn point: no place on 24 is out of a fresh Green's reach (24 is 134 wide; its farthest place from the point, 206, is 66 off, 0.23 s of its walk), but 206 is in 23's Hornies' reach (0.24), and the safest place on 24, 209-210 just past it (0.067), is only 0.005 safer than the spot, under `WAIT_GAIN`, and within `GROUND_MARGIN` of 24's left end besides: from 226 to 260, where the wait looks, it's 0.072, as at the spot (with the simulator's 15 x 55, as the model was built, 206 was 0.009 safer) (`DANGER_WEIGHT` and `WAIT_GAIN` are guesses, said so). Where a respawn lands on her is within its kind's half width and her 15 of its point (43 for the mushrooms; 50 with her 22, the client's), not 20: by that measure the live run at 68585b4 (below) stood within reach of a spawn point 94% of its waits (all four touches on 24 at x 218-264, 1-8% each), against 62-67% before.
- **Not leaving a ground for itself** (`hunt._elsewhere`): live at 68585b4 with 23 the only ground, "leaving platform 23: nothing came into reach" fired about once a second and 23 was picked again at once: 113 visits of about a second and no EXP went into the value map in 11 minutes. A reason to leave (an empty ground, her patience) is now waiting when `_next_ground` would take her nowhere else; it says so once ("staying at platform 23: ..."), and counts as progress as a new ground did, so the stuck guard fires as before.
- **Waiting on a rope** (`attack/ropes.py`, `hunt._refuge`; the user, 2026-10-24: "holding on a rope can also be a safe ground sometimes (less ideal because you can't recover hp while hanging on the rope)"). A rope is a place too: `Danger.on_rope` is the danger of hanging on one at a height, where a monster that walks its floor touches her only if its body, up to the highest its frames reach (`MapModel.mob_tall`: a slime's hop 66, where it stands 42-44; the mushrooms 52), meets hers, and a flier (`mob_flies`, a fly action: none on her maps) anywhere. She can get onto one from her platform and back from under it (its bottom within her grab reach, 15 in from her platform's ends, letting go to come down) or from its top (it hangs from her platform), at its safest height with 15 of slack for her dot and the climb, 0.5-1.5 s on and off. While she waits for respawns (rule 9), with this ground the only one or a kill of hers due back later than that, she goes up one only where it's `ROPE_GAIN` (0.05 of the HP she has left, a guess) safer than anywhere along her platform after its danger and what she doesn't regain there (`ropes.REGEN_S`, a guess at the game's 10 HP every 10 s, for `HANG_EXPECT_S`, the 17 s live median respawn), and its foot, where she comes back down to shoot, is no more than `WAIT_GAIN` more dangerous than the platform's safest place. Up there, whole-screen looks and potions as anywhere; she gets off for HP to rest at, the rope no safer than below, a monster she hunts with none a claw's swipe from where she lands (she lets it pass under her), time to go elsewhere, or a minute up (`HANG_MAX_S`). On Tree Dungeon no rope passes: 31's comes down 40 from its Horny Mushroom's spawn point (0.16 there, 0.12 at 31's safest place; taken anyway, she made 25% less EXP for the same HP, 31 alone in `crowd-17`, 8 seeds of 8 minutes), 10's by its two (0.23 against 0.18), the ones over 20 and 19 hang 1 and 6 units in from their ends (from 20's she came down on 21 in the simulator), and the rest aren't worth it over Green Mushrooms (gains 0.03-0.05). On a synthetic floor whose three Horny Mushrooms leave no safe place (`tests/test_ropes.py`, in the simulator) she waits on its rope and comes down to shoot: 8 seeds of 10 minutes, 158.9 EXP a minute against 154.3 without, 0.56 bars of HP a minute against 0.60, twice a run on the rope.

**The first map:** no kind counts as a danger there (`Danger.any` False), and spots, stance, waiting and the grounds' rates take none of the new paths: its 86 golden traces were byte-identical through the fights' change (`python -m gamelab.sim.golden check`); the waiting rule takes no new path there either, and wasn't checked against them, the work being tested on Tree Dungeon alone until it's carried to the other maps. On West Rocky Mountain II the Axe Stumps count: the stand spots weigh them (its scenarios' expectations hold); their keep-off, 87 (80 with the simulator's body), is inside the claw's reach (110), so the fights are as they were.

## Cutting her touches: her drops priced, the monsters the looks missed, edges, spots (from 2026-10-26)

The supervised hunt of 2026-10-25 (`tour --loop`, 23:43-23:54, 340 EXP a minute; lab/telemetry/20261025-234327-tour.jsonl, and the perception service's 10 fps records, 20261025-210747-perceive.jsonl), read through four lenses and each re-checked (D:/MapleHelper-scratch/analysis-1025/checked.txt): 34 touches, about 3.4 bars of her HP, 0.31 a minute (the simulator had 0.77). 23's Horny Mushrooms made 12 of them, 56% of the HP, all on 6 stays on 23 for drops, three of them ending in a knock off 23's left end, 389 units down to 29 (43 s back up); 24's Green Mushroom, on her spot's platform, 14. The detector is nearly blind near her: of the frames in which the service followed a monster on her platform, 7-12% found it within 50 units, 20-34% at 50-100, 44-55% beyond; none of the hunter's 1317 looks had one within 25. One Horny the looks missed (the service's track 130) made 6 touches and a fall: her trip's plan dropped her memory of it because 23 was in view, she jumped onto 23 beside it, and her pickup walk went through it.

What she does now (each a switch, on):
- **Her drops wait while a monster that counts is by them** (`hunt._pile_block`, `PILE_CLEAR` 100): no pile is gone for, walked to or stood on with a monster that counts as a danger within 100 units of the stretch from where she'd land (the route's last landing; where she stands on its platform) to the pile: one the look found, one about that it didn't (below), or a spawn point's monster due back, 5 to 12 s after a kill of hers there (`DUE_FROM`, `DUE_TO`). In rules 6a, 6, 5, 5b, 8 and 10; a trip for one is called off at the chain that sees it so, the pile kept. What's left of the danger there is the pile's price (`_pile_price`: `HP_EXP`, 250 EXP a bar of her HP, her pet's Orange Potions at a guessed 160 mesos) against what it's worth, EXP before loot (`_loot_first`). 100, not the analysis's 150: in the simulator (below, 8 seeds of 5 minutes) 0.87 bars a minute, 12.9 potions and 130 mesos a minute against 0.92, 13.6 and 115; on the live run's 78 pile decisions (scratch `b1_replay.py`) 150 has 47 wait and 100 44, and either way their stays had carried 7 touches and 1.04 bars.
- **Her decisions go by the monsters the looks missed** (`localization.unseen`; `hunt.UNSEEN_FIGHTS`, `navigator.UNSEEN_TRIPS`): the feed's tracks out of sight on screen (`Scene.tracks`, hidden, walked on as followed) and her memory's entries of the last 2 s, none the look found. Her trips keep clear of them (`Navigator.remembered` keeps those in view too; `hidden_tracks`; after a chain cut for a monster coming, its track for the next chain: `TripState.came`); her fights have them around her (the stance's danger and moves) and, those on her platform that count, as targets (rule 1 keeps off them); her drops are guarded by them (`Looter.unseen`), and a throw's attack goes by those on her platform (`Attacks.unseen`: Lucky Seven, not "nothing in its way"). On the live run the plan of her jump onto 23 at 23:47:42.63 would have kept the Horny her memory had at x 165 (1.8 s old, p 0.58): wherever it could be by then, -16 to 186, over her landing at 129; and the trip itself waited (above: unseen 0 units from her way).
- **Her escapes keep off a long drop's end, and may jump up onto the platform overhead** (`stance.drop_limits`, `DROP_ROOM` 100, `LONG_DROP` 100; `overhead`, `ESCAPE_UP`): her steps away and jumps back from monsters on her platform keep 100 from an end with the next platform under it 100 or more down (or none) and no wall of her foothold group there (23's left end: from -97; 24's ends and 23's right have short drops or the tower's wall); under a platform within her jump height the jump up onto it (a jump throw in place, "up onto 22") is an escape, by the danger where she'd land. Live, of her 17 steps away and jumps back toward 23's left end, the 6 that ended past -97 were the ones before all 3 falls (to -147 from -94 and -92, to -124 from -4: scratch `b3_replay.py`).
- **Before going past a monster regardless, a look and a shot** (`Navigator._last_shot`, `LAST_SHOT`): after the waits, a look with the monsters, and the blocking one shot if it's there and a star reaches it (live, 2 of the 4 touches within 3 s of a pass regardless came from a Green she had seen). In the simulator no difference past the seeds' spread; to watch live.
- **Spots off spawn points, and one that can touch her on her platform shot first** (`spots.SPAWN_CLEAR` 50, `_off_spawns`; `stance.TOUCHER_FIRST`): a spot keeps 50 from its platform's spawn points of kinds that count, where one reaching the ground does and it scores within that kind's touch share of the best (the knock room giving way at an end with a short drop, as far as it must): 23 from 24 at 221 (51 from 24's Green's point at 272, 97.9% of 23 in reach; it was 231, 41 off, where live she spent 172 of her 220 s on 24 within 50 units of it), 15 from 16 at 129; 7 stays shot from 8 at 147 (off its Green's point, 8's Horny's is nearer). Of the monsters in reach, one on her platform whose kind counts is thrown at first.
- **The simulator's potions as live** (`sim.world.PET_HP` 0.70, `HP_POTION` 250: her pet drank only under 70%, 6 times, 230-250 HP each) and **the detector's misses near her** (`--world near-misses`: `world.NEAR_SEEN`, a monster on her platform found never within 25 units, 7% within 50, 34% within 100, 55% within 150, by her not-attacking frames). The harness counts the touches on her stays for drops (`pile_hurt`, `pile_hp_lost`, `pile_s`: the loot rules and pile objectives), off her trips (`stand_hurt`), her knocks off a platform onto one 30 or more lower (`knock_falls`, `knock_fall_dy`: `World.knock_log`), and her pet's potions apart from her MP items (`pet_potions`, `mp_potions`).

## In game, on Thicket Around the Beach II

- **Navigation:** 21 moves in a row across every move type without a miss, and a 17-move climb from the basin to the top. Chained, a 13-move route up took 19.0 s in 3 key plans with no misses (33.4 s one move at a time). Over 10 test routes, a move landed elsewhere about once in 15, usually a drop or a straight-up jump timed off; each time she replanned from where she was.
- **Hops on routes:** across 4 routes (45 moves), she hopped 4 monsters in her way, with no hits and no misses.
- **Shooting from other platforms:** from platform 14 at monsters on 17 below, from 3 at monsters on 1 above, from 11 at 12 below and from 15 at 18 below. Most kills took 2 regular throws.
- **Sweeps:** a 6-stop sweep made 7 kills (EXP +92) in 114 s with no rest needed. With long-range shots, all 9 stops in 350 s with a rest: 28 kills (14 at the stops, 14 on the way) and EXP 6,459 → 6,856; far kills' drops were collected 5-50 s later. After the jump-back timing change, a full 9-stop sweep made 28 kills (6 on the way), EXP 6,856 → 7,247 in 309 s, lowest HP 92%. In crowds it took 7-9 throws a kill: each star hits whichever monster is nearest in its path, so the damage spreads.
- **Loot:** recorded at 2 frames a second, the sweep after a fight picked up a slime's Squishy Liquid and two coins within 8 s, and the tour's loot collection picked up drops seen from another platform. With her Etc tab full, a Snail Shell stayed after she stood on it twice; she read the game's message in the pickup log and switched to mesos only.
- **Arrows right after an attack** (2026-10-21; the telemetry of the hunt loops and tours played that night): an arrow pressed within 0.62 s of an attack's key (at least: later ones up to 0.78 s weren't pressed) doesn't move her until about 0.79 s after it. From 00:15 to 00:48, of 13 chains sent within 0.35 s of the look after a throw (their first arrow about 0.3 s after its key), 6 first moved her 0.73-0.92 s after they went out and 2 never did, and 9 missed their move: held on, the arrow set her off about 1.0 s after the key, and let go sooner it did nothing. Twice a walk-off off 3's left end onto 4, the arrow held 0.5 s from about 0.3 s after the key (00:19:04 and 00:19:37), left her standing on 3, and the walk-off sent again went. Arrows pressed 0.575 and 0.66 s after the key were read as moving her at once (but see below: by when she went off the end, the one at 0.575 set off 0.07 s late). From 00:49 every plan's first arrow waits until 0.6 s after the key (`actions.ARROW_LOCK_MS`): in the five runs to 01:17 no chain went sooner than 0.45 s after the look after a throw, all seven walk-offs from 3 onto 4 set off within 0.41 s, and every move that missed was a jump (9 of them: Motion planning), where before it 11 of the 13 were, and 2 were those walk-offs. The 5-minute hunt loop right after it ended 3 chains elsewhere than planned or off their path, the one before it 12. But held back to 0.6 s she still set off late, and those misses were its own: of the walk-offs from 3 onto 4, her dot first moved 0.41 s after the plan went out (the median of 11), against 0.23 s (of 6) when the first arrow came later after the key. Timed better, by when her dot went off the end of its platform (less the fall to her dot's first drop, and her run to the end by the engine), the 17 walk-offs whose first arrow went out 0.59-0.62 s after the key were acted on 0.75-0.88 s after it by her dot (median 0.81: 0.21 s after the plan went out, where an arrow with no attack before it is 0.09 s, the median of 73 walk-offs), so about 0.79 s after the key by the game's clock, 0.12 s late; the 3 whose first arrow went 0.79-0.82 s after the key were acted on at once (0.03-0.09 s). The press read as at once at 0.575 s was the walk-off at 00:19:14 (0.56 s after the key by this reckoning, the throw's key 0.13 s before the look after it): it set off 0.07 s late, and its dot first moved only as she fell. So she moves from about 0.79 s after the key, wherever up to 0.62 s the arrow went down; from 0.63 to 0.78 s no arrow was pressed, so whether one then is swallowed isn't known. **To check in game:** standing on open ground, `act f 650 right+ 500 right- 600 --record`, and the same with 700 and 750 for 650 (twice each, and left): she should start moving 66 ms after the arrow if it isn't swallowed, else about 0.79 s after the key; if 650 isn't, `actions.ARROW_LOCK_MS` can come down to it. Of the 10 chains that set off right after a throw for the jump from 16 up onto 14 (for the rope to 6), 8 came down short of 14 and fell to 25 (taking off 15-30 units short where tracked: the jump key went at its planned time) and one overshot onto 17 off a step back gone late; the 18 sent with no throw in the 1.5 s before all landed. (In the logs these read as rope grabs toward 6 that dropped her onto 25: 00:34:11, 00:46:42, 01:05:40, 01:05:58, 01:24:47, 01:44:06, 01:44:27, 01:48:40.) The first arrow now waits until 0.8 s after the key (Control), and the simulator acts on a swallowed one at about 0.79 s (`world.ARROW_HELD` 0.77 and the wait for the step; 0.835 at first, fitted to the dot's first move: with it, held back to 0.6 s the chains onto 14 were mostly cut short on 16 rather than falling to 25 as in game).
- **Jumps from standing** (2026-10-21, 34 jumps from platform 34 keyed 0-160 ms after the arrow): the game moves her in 30 ms steps, and a jump three steps into a run leaves at 126 units/s and lands 13 units short; from 150 ms after the arrow a jump leaves at full speed but for at most 5% (none of the 10 keyed 130 ms or later left short; of the 8 keyed 100 and 120 ms after it, 6 did). The kick: all 4 at 40 ms and 3 of 4 at 60 left at full speed, not reliable enough to plan on. Motion planning has the tables.
- **Respawns, and how full the map is** (the telemetry of 2026-10-19 to 21, offline: Respawns and drop life): a platform she emptied was mostly still empty 12.56 s later (of 77, watched without a gap over 2 s, 80% weren't back, 23 watched that long; past 30 s too few were watched to say; in the simulator about half were back within 5 s), and the platforms in view had a monster at 26% of their spawn points (the basin 12%, and none 45% of the time). Drops lay over a minute (95% at 60 s); how much longer, no hunt has shown.
- **Not yet checked on its own:** placing her from the minimap dot and the camera when her name tag is hidden.

## In game, on West Rocky Mountain II (map 10004021, from 2026-10-23)

The second map: Stump, Dark Stump, Axe Stump and Red Snail; a stone bridge (21, 22) over floating steps (23-25) down
into a pit of stairs (26-32), a higher bridge (11, 14) reached by a rope from 22, ledges above it (0-10) and to the
right (12-19, the way out to West Rocky Mountain I at 19). Her keys changed with it: attack Ctrl, Lucky Seven Shift,
and her pet drinks her potions (on C and V), so `hp_potion` and `mp_potion` are unbound and she feeds it (`pet_food`,
U) every `hunter.PET_FEED_S` (600 s; when she last did is kept in `lab/pet.json`). The bindings were checked in the
key bindings window (`act ''`). The simulator keeps her old keys (`timeline.SIM_KEYS`), so the golden traces don't
change with config.toml.

First look, explore only (no attacks):
- **Seen right:** her place from the minimap (`map --map 10004021`), HP and MP, her EXP number (31,736 at level 23,
  every glyph within 0.6 levels), and other players and their platforms.
- **Not seen:** the monsters. A kind is searched for only with a colour gate (`mobs.PALETTES`), and only the first
  map's four had one. Since 2026-10-24 its kinds have gates learned from its saved frames (Perception: learned gates).
- **Overlapping platforms:** 21 lies on 22's footholds and 11 on 14's, in other groups; she stands on either, and
  trips between them were planned as jumps and counted as landing elsewhere (4 of 8 moves on one trip). Found offline
  (Localization: Twins): they're in the same group as 22 and 14, 0.00-0.15 units above them, chains of their own
  joined into the long ones' footholds; each is now the long one for everything (her place, landings, routes,
  spawns), and the 26 moves between them are gone (8 + 8 jumps up onto them, 5 + 5 jumps and walk-offs off them).
- **The stairs:** she jumps onto a stair 60 up from the one below (from 30 to 29, 29 to 28, 28 to 27), but the
  planner has no route out of the pit ("no route from platform 30 to 19"), and from 27 to the floating step 25 (44
  across, 60 up) she twice fell back to 28. Found offline (Motion planning): every stair is jumped up (17 units to
  spare under her 77-unit jump, the riser a wall of her own group cleared from 15-35 units back); the one way out,
  27 onto 25, lands only from the last 17 units of 27 at full speed, too short a stretch for a sure jump, so the
  pit had none. It's now a way out of a trap (`route._ways_out`, 0.90 by her takeoff's spread; the mover does it on
  its own, closed loop); in the simulator, with the game's errors, it landed 16 of 17 tries on 16 seeds.
- **Her x from the minimap** read 32-43 units left of the stair she stood on (y 10 low), three times. Found offline
  (Localization): the minimap's picture sits 5 game px further left in this map's window than in the first map's,
  where the dot's origin was fitted, so every x her dot gave read 80 units left (on the floating step 24 -1026 for
  the -938 two ropes in view put her at; on stair 29 -961, off it); her y was right (24's 2505, 30's 2805 to 3). The
  dots are now read through the picture, found on the looks (`minimap.MinimapPicture`), at 1920×1080 and at 1366×768
  (on stair 28: -880 before, -800 now, the ropes' -792). Replayed with the 80 units put back, today's trips are
  plain: from 24 the jump up to 23 and the jump on to 22 went 80 units right of where they were planned (she was at
  -946, not -1026), came down on 24 and 23, and the fourth try, planned from 23 at its real place, landed on 22.
- **Ropes:** the map has four (at x -414, -1463, -536, -303); the climb from 14 to 7 missed and ended on 17. Found
  offline: her dot's 80 units (above). The chain ran her right along 14 from where her dot put her, and the jump at
  the rope, keyed for 41 units before it (-455), came when she was 80 units further on, past 14's end (-406): she ran
  off it and the key came in the air (both times, her dot corrected: -402 then down onto 17 at -354). The rope itself
  is grabbed as planned (its bottom 64 above 14 there, platform 10 between them, 20 under it; missing it from -455
  lands on 10, not off 14), and in the simulator the climb arrives on 16 seeds of the game's errors.
- Full-size frames from 24, the bridge (22), the high bridge (11), 17 and the stairs (30) are in
  `D:/MapleHelper-scratch/newmap/frames` (`<platform>_<n>.png`).

## In game, on Tree Dungeon, Forest Up North II (map 10002073, from 2026-10-24)

The third map, "supposedly better for exp hunting", with many more other players: Horny Mushrooms (15 spawn points, HP
403, EXP 41, a touch's PADamage 110), Green Mushrooms (14, HP 233, EXP 26, PADamage 54) and two Slimes on the floor.
It is a tower 530 units wide (x -220..310) between two walls of foothold group 0, from the floor (33, y 248..225) up
to 6 (y -1628): shelves of group 0 against either wall (left 7, 13, 15, 20, 22, 29, 31; right 6, 12, 19, 24, 28) and
floating platforms of other groups between them, none with a wall; platforms 0-5 are wall caps above the VR, never
reached. Two exits (`out00` at 282 on 28, `out01` at 281 on 12) lead to 10002070. The map is 770 wide, so the camera
can't move across (Localization), and its minimap window shows 112 of its picture's 183 rows, scrolled with her. She's
level 25 (HP 774, MP 623, Lucky Seven at 16: 14 MP a cast; level 24's HP 752 and MP 601 until 2026-10-26: Her throws at the dead), with West Rocky Mountain II's keys and pet. The read-only study of 2026-10-24 has the rest
(`D:/MapleHelper-scratch/map3/study/ONBOARDING.md`: structure, grounds, stands, monsters and drops, crowding, the 20
native frames hand-labelled). Its monsters and drops as the stack sees them are under Perception (learned gates, kinds
that share a body, drops per map); its motion and the simulator there are below.

**The motion** (offline, from the game data and the planner: 175 moves, 74 jumps, 39 drops, 36 walk-offs, 13
jump-ups, 7 climbs, 6 climb-downs; every place gets to every other but the caps):
- **7 (two Horny Mushrooms) is reached:** the running jump off 8's left end (x 22), 45 up and 28 across, lands only
  from takeoffs at x 24-48, 2-26 units in from the end (the study's brute force); the jumps off an end looked for
  nearer the end (`route.END_LOOK`, West Rocky Mountain II's 17 onto 16) run from x 57, take off at x 37 (15 in) and
  land at -32. 25 -> 29 is found the same way. In the simulator 10 -> 7 by 9 and 8 arrives in 4 s
  (`nav_tree_top_cluster`).
- **The rope at -8 from 30** hangs 94.3 above 30's floor: 0.7 inside `physics.ROPE_REACH` (95, a guess from the start;
  the highest grab tried in game was 69 up) and 12.3 beyond HeavenClient's (`Ladder::inrange`: a point 5 above her feet
  on the rope, 82 at the top of her jump, `physics.GRAB_SURE`). It was on every way up from the bottom. A climb up a
  rope beyond a sure grab now lands half the time (`route.GRAB_CHANCE`, each miss 3 s), so the ways up go 30 -> 29 (a
  running jump) and up the same rope from 29 (36.8 up): 2.7 s against 2.4. Missed, the jump for it passes up through 29
  (58 above 30 there) and lands her on it; in the simulator with HeavenClient's reach (`Realism.rope_reach`) she does,
  and goes on up from there (`nav_tree_rope_miss`). No other climb on the three maps is between the two reaches.
- **Its floors slope** (most floating ones 17-19 units over 188, 0.1 a unit; the floor 33 rises 248 to 225 to the
  right): a step onto a floor rising the way she flew went through it, by the engine's landing check (Motion
  planning: now as HeavenClient judges it). Knocked right off 30 she came down through the floor and out of the map;
  of six 5-minute hunt loops from 28 four fell out, two inside the tower; none of those now.
- **The tower's walls** (group 0) don't stop her off a floating platform's outer end or a rope, by the client's
  movement as reconstructed (`body.OWN_GROUP_WALLS`; off a rope, none: `body.ROPE_GROUP`): knocked off 11's left end
  (13 from the wall), 23's (23), 25's right end (34), 10, 26, 17 or 8 toward the wall, or off the rope at -192 (28 from
  it), she falls past it and out of the map. Whether the game stops her there isn't known: `Realism.zero_walls` has
  group 0's walls stop her whatever her group, off a rope too, and the harness runs both (`--world zero-walls`).
  HeavenClient has neither rule: a wall stops her if it's chained to the foothold below her (its prev or the one
  before, `FootholdTree::get_wall`), whatever its group; that's a third reading, not modelled.
- **The exits:** no plan holds up within 60 units of a portal, nor on a platform up to 125 above an exit
  (`MapModel.portal_near`: flow and the mover's climbs; by one, the mover's climbs press up only after the jump's key,
  `mover.PORTAL_UP_MS`, which helps only if the game takes a portal on the ground alone), and no rope hangs near either
  exit. HeavenClient takes a portal while up is held with her feet from 100 above it to 25 below and 25 either side,
  on the ground or in the air (`Stage::update`, `Portal::bounds`, read 2026-10-24; `mapdata.PORTAL_BOX`), and so does
  the simulator: there it takes her out of the map and ends the run (`world.SimLeftMap`). One harness run of an
  earlier build did, a chain played on after a knock the stack didn't see (Harness baselines, below).
