- The simulator's world stops the star there too (`World.nearest_in`, by the group of the platform she last stood on), the claw's swing as well. New scenario `fight_step_wall`: on 4 at x 520 with Orange Mushrooms on 2 and nothing on 4. With the old decisions in this world she threw 23 stars in 20 s and hit nothing; now she throws none and moves on after 1.7 s.
- The shot log keeps her x, the target's x, her group and the rule's verdict (`clear`), so the probes can be scored from it. A monster seen past its platform's end is placed at the end (`Scene.place`).
- In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against before: the world's rule alone took the tour from 89.5 to 88.4 EXP a minute and the hunt loop from 122.4 to 120.8; with the decisions too, 87.5 and 122.1. In each, 9 of 10 seeds are the same throughout. The one that differs is a run that met 4 -> 2, diverging from there (tour seed 8: 104.3, then 92.8, then 83.5, where she went up onto 2 instead and was knocked back down 10 times; loop seed 7: 71.9, 55.4, 68.5). On this map the effect is within noise; on maps with steps it should be larger.
- Not measured in game yet: the line's height (anything from 1 to 59 fits), its height in a jump (a jump throw's attack starts 26 up, its line under the 60 step's top; a jump back's 44 up, over it), whether the claw's swing is stopped too, and whether it's a level line at all and not one from her to the target's feet (they differ shooting down from 2 at 4 from far left of the step, and down past her own block's hanging wall).

**The star's search, and the angle off her level** (from the game data, meowdb and the live logs, 2026-10-20; see Attack). Off her level she judged a star's reach by one ramp for every monster (150 units sideways 60 below her, 200 above), and the simulator by a box. The logs fit the client's search by bands better, and by it the room a star needs goes by the monster's body (60 below her, an Orange Mushroom from 113 units, a slime from 177). Now:
- `reach` has the search (`search_band`, `min_dx` and `max_dx` by body, `body_of`), and her decisions go by it with each monster's kind (`can_shoot`, `can_hit`, `shot_dx`): `stance` and `decide` (with their steps away from one right under her, and a walk to one band past where a star picks it), `pick_target`, the fight's far targets, the hunt loop's rules, the tour's stop on the way, the escort and `hunt look`. Kill matching too (`fight._in_path`, `ledger.in_path`, `reach.ground_pick`): from the ground what the claw (on her level) or the search can pick, in the order it takes them; from a jump the area to 400 by a body's near side. Spots go by a pig's body (12 from 13: 60% -> 63% in reach).
- The simulator's world picks by it (`World.nearest_star`), the claw only on her level, and its stars reach 400 to a body's near side from the ground or a jump (434 for a pig by its middle; 494 before).
- Step 1's walls fit with it. Of her throws from 4 at monsters on 2 (60 above, 286-392 units off), the search alone picks one on 2 every time; with the walls, none. On the research's fits rerun against `reach`, the search scores -55.52 (shot log, off her level), -42.49 (telemetry, the nearest monster in front), -82.22 (telemetry, the whole pick) and -64.06 (her level); with the walls, -54.09 on the shot log (its 3 misses from 4 at 2) and the same on the rest, since the walls change no other pair. On the terrain research's scoring (did a throw from the ground hurt anything, 195 decided throws): the old rule -116.70, with the walls -116.40; the search -116.44, with the walls -116.24.
- In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against before: the world alone (her old decisions) took the tour from 87.5 to 85.9 EXP a minute and the hunt loop from 122.1 to 111.3; with the decisions too, 85.2 and 121.4 (better on 3 and 5 of the 10 seeds; in the new world against her old decisions, on 4 and 5, with 3 of the loop's the same). HP lost 2.64 -> 2.62 bars (tour), 2.51 -> 2.42 (loop); drops picked up 95.9% either way (tour), 83.9% -> 79.8% (loop, nearly all of it seeds 2 and 4, where monsters are always in reach). Attacks that hit nothing: tour 666 -> 869 -> 786, loop 917 -> 1,114 -> 1,072. Most of the rise is stars at targets 400-470 off by their near side, aimed at within 410 by their middle and walked out of reach by the time the attack started: the shorter range makes the world harder, and the decisions win back part of it. Aiming within 400 instead would cut those by another 4-5% (tour 87.7, loop 121.2 EXP a minute); it's left at 410, where the range probe hit slimes at 408 and 410, until it's measured in game.
- `tour_run` moved from seed 8 to 6, and its limit to 1200 s: the basin's respawns make it chaotic (470-1190 s on seeds 1-16, before and after), and on seed 8 one EXP gain came in as the stop's fight ended, unplaced.
- The shot log is `lab/shots2.jsonl` from now on (`hunt shots` sums up both). The old one made every throw in flight at a kill a hit (its hits 60 below at 91-148 were mostly that). Now a kill is the last throw due before the target died (its HP bar gone after it had one) or else vanished; those due before it are undecided, those due after it missed. A star is due from when its key went down (held back to the end of the attack lock, up to 0.7 s after it was decided), and each throw keeps every monster in front of her at the throw and at the next look, about when the attack picked its target. A throw is scored from the last HP bar seen before its star was due, not the bar at the throw: keyed before the star ahead of it landed, it was credited with that one's hit (`hunt_kill_misses`: its second throw, a miss in the world, was logged a hit). In `hunt_kill_far` the log now reads hit, hit, hit, miss, miss, as the world has it (five hits before).
- Not measured in game yet: the windows' top (`SEARCH_TOP0`), the range to a body's near side, whether the search goes by each monster's frame (a slime's hop lifts its body 10-17; a pig stands 38 tall, walks 46), and how an attack from a jump picks its target.

**The claw or a star, by the monster's size** (from the game data and the simulator, 2026-10-20; see Attack). `stance` and the hunt loop took a monster on her platform as too close for a throw from the ground within 100 units of her by its middle, and `decide` within 110, whatever its kind. But the claw swipes any body that reaches into its swing, so pigs, slimes and Orange Mushrooms 100-124 units off were swiped (9-11 damage) where she meant to throw a star. Now:
- `reach.claw_edge` is the swing's reach (90) plus half the monster's width plus 12 to spare: a pig 136, an Orange Mushroom 134, a slime 130, a snail 121. `stance` (`close_to`), the hunt loop's close quarters (rule 1) and `decide` go by it, with each kind's width from the map's game data. Her step back for a star at a kind a jump's attack can't pick goes to 3 past it (`reach.star_sure`: a snail's is 124, as before), and `decide` walks to 20 past it (it was 150 for every kind).
- In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against the search above: the tour 85.2 -> 89.2 EXP a minute (better on 6 of the 10 seeds, the worst -12%), the hunt loop 121.4 -> 126.3 (better on 9; seed 7, the chaotic one, 73.2 -> 39.3: from 136 s on it takes another course, and spends 770-1150 s going back and forth between the basin and piles on 21 and 22). Claw swipes fell by more than a third (tour 1,310 -> 803, loop 1,831 -> 1,188), and kills by the claw by half (36 -> 17, 65 -> 29). HP lost 2.62 -> 2.52 bars (tour), 2.42 -> 2.13 (loop); drops picked up 95.9% -> 94.4% (tour), 79.8% -> 76.6% (loop). It doesn't pass the harness bar (the seed count, and the drops share). The bodies research measured the same change in the world before the search and the walls: swipes -35%, EXP a minute +1.3% (tour) and +5.6% (loop) over 20 seeds.
- 21 golden scenarios changed: pigs and slimes 100-136 units off on her platform get a jump back or a jump throw instead of a throw from the ground, and a single target that close (`hunt_kill_misses`, a pig 119 off) a step away. Those of the basin's fights end sooner or kill more (`tour_hunt_noisy` its 14 kills in 109 s instead of 272; `nav_fight_basin` 26 kills on the way instead of 20).
- The ledger's placements in the basin (`tests/test_ledger.py`) are as right as before on average (seeds 1-10 of its three scenarios: 72-78% against 73-77%), but on `nav_fight_basin`'s own seed 15 of 24 instead of 16 of 19, so the test now asks for 60% of them, not 65%.
- Not measured in game: how far off the claw still swipes a pig (by the swings' boxes, 107-136 by its middle; `probe run claw-edge`), and whether it swipes off her level (`probe run claw-down`).

**A kill her EXP told of as a fight ended went uncounted** (simulator, 2026-10-20). A fight tells its kills apart from her EXP gains look by look, but the looks of a sweep for drops count gains too, and when that brought the fight to its kill count it ended without telling the last one apart: the kill was missing from its kills by kind, which a tour's stop sums up (in one `tour_run` of 161 kills, 34 gains came that way, and the 2 of them at its stops were missing; the escort counts by gains, not kinds). Now `fight.clear` tells those apart from its last look when it ends, and those seen by the look it ends on (knocked off), and then those its sweep after the fight sees, counted but with their drops left: left to the tour, they sent it on loot trip after loot trip (`tour_run`: 16 instead of 6, 1185 s instead of 708). `tour_run`'s "every kill counted" holds on 13 of seeds 1-16 instead of 5; the rest are 1-2 kills short of 150-215. 8 golden scenarios changed by a kill's log line ("pig down at x 805") and the sweep of its drops, and from there on (`nav_fight_across`, `tour_run`, `tour_rest`, `tour_rest_hp_low`, `tour_hunt_noisy`, `tour_hunt_basin`, `tour_hunt_platform_12`, `loop_basin`); with those seen by the look a fight ends on, `nav_fight_up` and `tour_hunt_platform_12` by one more such line; with those of the sweep after it, 15 more by such a line and nothing else (`fight_snail_short_platform`'s report now has the slime its sweep saw die: "1 snail, 1 slime", 2 EXP gains as before).

**Kills fade out as long as their death animation runs** (game data, 2026-10-20). A killed monster plays its die1 frames where it died, the last one fading it out: 1.26 s for a snail, 0.84 for a slime, 0.66 for a pig or an Orange Mushroom. The fight left whatever it saw where a kill had died alone for 1.2 s whatever the kind, and the simulator showed every kill for 0.8 s. Now both go by the kind (`MobBody.die_s`: the death animations are read with the map and kept in its cache under their own key, so older code still reads it; `fight.fade_s`, `SimMonster.fade`), so a pig walking up to where one has just died is shot at half a second sooner. 19 golden scenarios changed besides `nav_fight_up`, as a ghost shows longer or shorter and she turns to a live one sooner; each still shows what it's there for. `nav_fight_up` moved from seed 10 to 3: on some seeds a touch knocks her off 3 or 4 down to the basin and the trip runs out of time, more often since the claw's edge (she arrives on 38 of seeds 1-48 with the decisions of the star search, 34 with the claw's edge, 30 with this too, seed 10 no longer among them). In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against the commits before it: the tour 88.7 -> 87.5 EXP a minute; the hunt loop 126.6 -> 115.5, all of it seed 10, which fell out of the map (see Found offline) - without it 126.9 -> 126.8. HP lost 2.58 -> 2.47 bars (tour), 2.12 -> 1.95 (loop); drops picked up 95.2% -> 94.4% (tour), 76.7% -> 68.4% (loop; 74.4% -> 69.5% without seed 10, from seeds 7 and 9). Against the star search (before the claw's edge): tour 85.2 -> 87.5, loop 121.4 -> 115.5 (121.3 -> 126.8 without seed 10), claw swipes 1,310 -> 861 and 1,831 -> 1,046, HP lost 2.62 -> 2.47 and 2.42 -> 1.95.

**42 below her, the search was taken on trust** (review of the search, 2026-10-20; see Attack). The search's numbers between her level and 60 off it were stated as measured, but nothing was thrown 42 above her, and the only throws logged 42 below go against them: from 0 at 1, pigs 124-125 off missed 3 times of 4 and an Orange Mushroom 91 off missed, where the search picks both from 90 (by the old ramp, 126). Now:
- Between her level and 60 below her, her decisions keep at least to the old ramp (`reach.RAMP_BELOW`, `ramp_dx`: 42 below, 126 for every kind; a snail's 166 by the search), until `probe run below-42` settles it. Kill matching and the simulator's world still go by the search. On this map only 0 -> 1 is that far apart.
- New probes for the claw too, which step 3 changed on the simulator's word alone: `claw-edge` (how far off the claw still swipes on her level, `reach.CLAW_FAR` and `CLAW_MARGIN`) and `claw-down` (whether it swipes off her level). A swipe is told from a star by how much of the bar it takes (`probe.CLAW_DROP`, 15%: 9-11 damage against 30-70, of a pig's 128 HP and a slime's 115 by the game data).
- In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against before: the tour the same on every seed; the hunt loop only on seed 9, which starts on 1 below 0 and takes another course from there (96.0 -> 99.4 EXP a minute, drops picked up 62% -> 98%: the drops it lost lay on 17, far from 0): 115.5 -> 115.8, 68.4% -> 72.0%. The golden traces are the same (no scenario throws from 0 at 1).

**The shot log gave one throw's hit to another** (simulator, 2026-10-20). A bar drop was a hit for every throw past its due time, however late the look, and the fight looks once a throw, right after each key: the look that should have shown a throw's miss often showed the next one's hit (`hunt_kill_misses`: its sixth throw, a miss in the world, was logged a hit). A kill went to the last throw due before the target was seen dying, but the simulator shows a star's damage at its release (520 ms after the key) whatever the distance, so a far throw that killed wasn't due yet and was logged a miss. Which of the two the game does isn't measured: a star is seen flying up to 0.6 s to its target, but the bar may follow the attack's message to the server. Now:
- A throw's damage can show from 0.47 s after its key to 0.3 s past its due time. A bar drop is a throw's hit only if no other throw's can have shown since the look before it, and a kill is the last throw's that can have landed only if that one alone can, or it was due by then; else they're undecided. Seen with its bar as before 0.3 s past its due time (0.9 s before), a throw missed.
- A throw's pick is the first look after it that places her, whether or not the target is in it (hidden under its damage numbers, it was noted at the next look it showed in, once 1.7 s after the star was due), with its time from the key. Each line keeps the target's bar at every look until it was scored and the throw's key time, so a scorer that knows the game's timing can tell the undecided ones.
- Over seeds 1-12 of the four `hunt_kill` scenarios, with hit monsters hidden for 1-3 s and without (the 63 runs that log every star the world throws, 279 throws): before, 253 right, 15 misses logged as hits, 2 kills as misses, 9 undecided; now 211 right, none wrong, 68 undecided. Of those, the 19 from the runs without hiding are all told right from their bars by the simulator's timing; the other 49 landed while the target was hidden, and nothing tells those apart.
- Golden: the shot logs of `hunt_kill`, `hunt_kill_far`, `hunt_kill_misses` and `hunt_kill_tag_hidden` (in `hunt_kill_misses` the miss logged a hit and the hit after it are undecided, and a miss that was undecided is a miss).

Seed by seed, master -> all three (EXP a minute): the tour 100.7 -> 88.0, 102.2 -> 87.3, 82.4 -> 79.2, 100.8 -> 89.7, 85.5 -> 93.5, 92.5 -> 85.8, 80.5 -> 86.8, 94.0 -> 103.5, 84.2 -> 78.8, 86.0 -> 91.7; the hunt loop 134.3 -> 127.8, 146.4 -> 148.1, 102.5 -> 86.8, 160.9 -> 153.3, 139.8 -> 108.6, 160.3 -> 153.4, 82.9 -> 62.8, 138.8 -> 123.3, 101.2 -> 108.9, 124.5 -> 125.0. The world got harder, as the speeds commit found on the perception track alone (there the tour 89.5 -> 84.2 and the loop 123.0 -> 122.9, HP lost up a fifth): monsters walk 1.8 times as fast, touch her 18% (tour) and 37% (loop) more often, and gain on her through the hits' push-backs (a pig shot every 0.85 s gains 13 units a hit, where at 70 units/s it lost 0.5). Neither passes the harness bar against master, which judges policies; here the world changed, and the decisions tuned against slow monsters (hop timing, `TOO_CLOSE`, the star/claw boundary) aren't retuned yet. Loop seed 7 (138.9 -> 82.9 at the review) is the worst again. It wasn't chaos: there the loop locked onto the basin. Its first visit lasted 351 s, which made the basin worth 136 EXP a visit and put every other ground under the 15% of that the loop kept grounds by (the best, 17, at 19.5), so twice, leaving the basin empty, she went straight back to it, and spent 1,150 of 1,200 s on a basin holding one slime (57.6 EXP a minute); master's code does it on seeds 11 and 19. The loop now keeps its grounds by their EXP a second (`docs/hunt-modes.md`): seed 7 leaves for 4 at 351 s and makes 62.8, seeds 5 and 16 leave the basin for 17 once (108.6 and 130.7, from 114.5 and 131.3); over seeds 1-30 the mean is 123.0 against 123.1, its drops picked up 89.0% against 90.5% (seed 7's: 53% away from the basin, against all of them in it). Seed 7 stays the worst for what she meets on her way, not for the lock.

With the feed (`--feed --costs measured`, `fin-loop-feed-measured`) against blocking looks as fast as they're measured (`--costs measured`, `fin-loop-measured`), the hunt loop 127.3 -> 131.0 EXP a minute (better on 8 of 10 seeds, the worst -7.8%; +3.1% on average, standard error 1.7%), drops picked up 90.7% -> 83.4%, HP lost 2.76 -> 2.49 bars, rests 1.9 -> 2.0 a run; the feed looked at 24.9 frames a second (busy 71% of the time), and its tracker judged 2,036 deaths hers (she killed 2,164) and 120 someone else's (the rival killed 467). On the perception track alone, over 20 seeds, the loop made 3% less with the feed; here it makes 3% more, but picks up fewer of its drops, so it doesn't pass the bar and `--feed` stays off. (Against the default blocking looks, coded at 100 ms, 119.8 -> 131.0. Before the loop kept its grounds by their EXP a second and the tracker's throws reached 474, not 540, `perc-combined-loop-*`: 125.7 -> 131.9, +6.0%, standard error 3.1%, better on 9; seed 7's blocking run, locked onto the basin, made 88.3 where it now makes 104.5.)

Checked and kept: after a plan is cut short, the quick look has no monsters, so fighting on the way (and `Tour.hunt`'s stop when a target is in reach) is skipped for one chain. That lets her move on from wherever a hit knocked her first. With a look that sees the monsters there instead, she fought wherever she was knocked to: `nav_fight_across` (then 12 s from 12 across to 17) ran out its 180 s (30 kills, knocked off 12 times, once all the way down to 32), and `tour_hunt_platform_12` took 79 s longer.

**The client's own target search and close-range stab** (cross-validation against public readings of the client's code, 2026-10-20; see Attack: where the search and the claw's reach come from). `reach` had the search's top fitted to the logs and the claw's reach from its swings' boxes; both now follow the client's routine, and so do her decisions, kill matching and the simulator's world:
- The search's window is the client's, 28 ± (65 + 20k) / 4 (`reach.window`, `spread`): its top 44 + 5k above her feet, not the logs' 21 + 5k; the floor, 12 - 5k, as before. A pig 60 above her is picked from 111 units, not 191 (an Orange Mushroom from 113, a slime from 117, a snail from 127), and one 42 above as on her level; from 13, 86% of 12 is in reach instead of 63%. `reach.SEARCH_TOP0` is still the one number to change.
- The claw's close range is its two stab stances, one per attack at random, each box cut to 65 ahead of her (`reach.CLAW_FAR`, 90 before), on her level or not (`claw_takes` by stance, `claw_chance`): on her level a pig is swiped out to 99 by its middle, not 124. `reach.claw_edge` follows (a pig 111, an Orange Mushroom 109, a slime 105, a snail 96, from 136-121), and with it `star_sure` (a snail's 99, from 124), `stance.close_to` and the hunt loop's close quarters; on her level she counts on a star from 65 plus half its width (`reach.shot_dx`; a flat 90 before). The old ramp below her keeps its 90 on her level (`reach.RAMP_NEAR`).
- Within a band any monster can take the star: the simulator draws one per attack, and the ledger takes every monster a throw can have hit as its possible kill (`reach.take_chances`, `ledger.in_path`), preferring one gone from view. The fight's kill matching (`fight._in_path`) already counted every monster in a star's path. The simulator draws the claw's stance per attack too (both seeded).
- In the simulator's game-like world (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes), against the review's runs (`review-fixed-*`, which the branch before this reproduces exactly): the tour 90.9 -> 96.4 EXP a minute (better on 6 of 10 seeds, the worst -12%), kills 162.5 -> 172.9 a run, drops picked up 94.7% -> 92.6%, HP lost 2.26 -> 2.17 bars; the hunt loop 129.2 -> 130.3 (better on 4 of 10, the worst -10%, the best +18%), drops 87.8% -> 92.2%, HP lost 1.93 -> 2.18 bars, with touches 271 -> 291 a run (she counts on a star from closer, so she stands closer). Neither passes the harness bar. The world moved as much as her decisions, since it's the model of the game: a world with the old geometry would have to be kept to tell them apart.
- 21 golden scenarios changed, each still showing what it's there for. The claw's shorter reach: stars where the world swiped at 99-129 units, and throws from the ground at 100-136 where she jumped back (`hunt_kill`, `hunt_clear_basin`, `nav_fight_basin`, `tour_hunt_onto_platform`, `tour_rest`, `tour_rest_hp_low`, `loop_spot_off_ground`, `loop_rest_hp_low`, `loop_run`, `loop_noisy`). The higher top: `probe_top` (every throw hits, 127-192 off), `tour_hunt_platform_12` (86% of 12 in reach from 13) and `loop_lull_loot`, moved from seed 6 to 4 (on 6 no lull came; over seeds 1-16, 6 meet all of it, 8 before). The draw within a band: `nav_fight_up` (an Orange Mushroom 219 off took the star a pig 238 off had), `tour_hunt_from_safe_spot` (a pig 355 off, an Orange Mushroom's star before) and `tour_hunt_platform_12`. The rest follow from those in the basin (`tour_hunt_basin`, `tour_hunt_noisy`, `tour_run`, which ends its three stops in 635 s instead of 964, `loop_basin`, `loop_leaves_empty_ground`, `loop_rival`).
- Not measured in game: the window's top (`probe run top-60`), how far off the claw still swipes (`probe run claw-edge`: by the stabs a pig to 99 and a slime to 93, by the swings to 124 and 118), and whether it swipes below her level (`probe run below-42`: an Orange Mushroom 42 below is in the stab boxes only in its frames taller than 57; `claw-down` tells only the swings from the stabs). The air pick and what stops a star are as before.

**What stops a star, checked on the pick alone** (cross-validation against public readings of the client's code, 2026-10-20; see Attack: what stops a star). The simulator's pick passed a walled monster over and gave the star to the next, walls stopped a star going either way, and the claw's swipe was stopped by them too. Now, as the client does it:
- The claw and the star's search pick by the bodies, whatever the terrain, and the line to a star's pick alone is checked (`World.sight_stop`). Stopped, the attack hits nothing: the star ends there (the world's log says at which wall or floor), and no other monster takes it. A swipe isn't checked: the client's whack has no line. Her decisions take a monster as out of a star's reach when one the attack takes first has its star stopped (`reach.shielded`, through `reach.can_hit`'s `others`, the monsters around her: in `stance`, `decide` and `pick_target`, the fight's far targets, the hunt loop's rules, the tour's stop on the way, the escort and `spots.spot_for`), and kill matching does so too (`fight._in_path`, `ledger.in_path`). `hunt look` says "behind one behind a wall" for such a monster.
- A wall stops a star only going in at its outer face (`reach.faces`: a wall drawn downward faces right, one drawn upward left; `reach.clear_span` and the mini-map's band, for which the page now gets each wall's face). On this map every wall faces out from the platform it hangs from (a test checks all 105), so this changes nothing here.
- The v83 client's line is there behind `reach.LINE = 'v83'`, for when the probes find it: `reach.hit_point`, `reach.sight_stop` (every floor of the map, `Platform.floors`, and the footholds joined to each one's ends, `Segment.prev` and `next`, from the map image, for the corners), `reach.clear_length` for `spots.coverage`. Where a floor stops the star from where she'd walk to, `stance` and `decide` walk her along her platform to the nearest place, 10 units apart, from where it doesn't (`stance._clear_from`; a wall of her group stops it from all of them, so the level line changes nothing there).
- With the level line none of it changes what she does on this map. In the harness (preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes) every seed gives the same EXP, drops and HP as before: the tour 96.4 EXP a minute, the hunt loop 130.3. Of the 66 golden traces one changed, `probe_step_up`, where the world's log now says that the 6 stars thrown from the ground at an Orange Mushroom on 2 stopped at the step wall (x 219), not "no hit". Only 4 -> 2 is walled here, and from 4 a monster on her side of the wall comes first in the search: over the 10 seeds the tour's world stopped 1 star of 6,264 at the wall and the loop's 2 of 8,240, and before, those hit nothing either.
- What the line is worth here, in the simulator: on the tour, over 30 seeds, 95.3 EXP a minute with the level line in the world and her decisions, 95.9 in a world with the v83 line and her decisions on the level line, 95.2 with both on the v83 line, and 93.2 with her decisions on the v83 line in a level world (a seed moves 20-25% either way, so these are good to a few percent). In a v83 world her level decisions lose 47 of 6,244 stars to a floor (0.8%, 10 seeds), and decisions on the v83 line still 26 (the world's monsters aren't quite where her last look had them); the loop loses 1 of 8,256, and it makes the same 130.3-130.5 EXP a minute whichever line the world and her decisions go by. So on this map the line is worth under 1% of her stars, whichever it is.
- Found offline, not fixed: on the tour's seed 28, with her decisions on the v83 line (in either world), the last 260 s went by without a kill. A chain capped at its length mid-climb (from 17 by 14 and 6 up to 3, `chain ended: long`) left her on the rope by 6, and the escort, from a look that put her on a platform, threw at monsters "in reach" again and again: the world takes no attack on a rope. It's the escort's (`FightOnTheWay`, `navigator._shoot_on_the_way`), not the line's; with the level line that seed never got there.
- Not measured in game: which line (`probe run step-down` and `hanging-wall`, as the next entry placed them: Probes to run; the user saw her own platform stop her stars on 2026-10-21, below); where a stopped star's picture ends (a recording of a throw from 4 at 2: by v83 at the step wall, about 300 units out, not 400); whether walls of group 0 stop a star from every group (RustMS's port alone; here only the basin is group 0, where it would change nothing).

**The probes weigh both answers** (2026-10-20, after the two above; see Probes to run). `probe score` fitted the window's top and floor and compared terrain models by which monster each attack took, the nearest first; of the claw it had only a table of bar drops, and step-down and hanging-wall still asked a level line against a line to the target's feet. Now:
- Each open question is between reach's answer and one other (`probe.QUESTIONS`): the top (44 / 21), the claw on her level (the stabs cut to 65 / one box to 90, on her level only), the claw off it (the stabs / the throw's swings), the ramp below her (the search alone / the ramp), the line (level / v83), her own group's walls (they stop a star / none does), other groups' walls (they don't / every wall does), and the pick from a jump (the area / the v83 client's, as from the ground from as high as she is). Each answer gives every monster in front at the pick its chance of a swipe and of a star (`probe.chances`, as the simulator's world picks: a stance per attack, any monster of the search's first band, the line to the pick alone), and the likelihood weighs each hit's size too: the claw's under 20 HP's worth of the bar, by the kind's HP, so a snail's swipe (20-24% of its bar) is no longer read as a star's, as the 15% threshold had it. From a jump the attack is taken to start at either height it may (JUMP_LAG after its key or 11 ms later: 21 or 26 up for a jump throw), each as likely.
- The probes stand where their throws tell the answers apart under the client's pick: step-down at pigs 200-255 and slimes 200-285 (no Orange Mushrooms: all of 4's are past where the lines differ), hanging-wall from x 706 (was 730) at pigs and slimes 180-240, claw-down at pigs only within 100 (40 throws), below-42 at Orange Mushrooms only from 100 (past the stabs' boxes, which its taller frames reach).
- So the simulator can have the other answers: `reach.Claw` and `reach.CLAW` (the claw's stances, their cut and a level gate, as a model the world and her decisions go by), `reach.attack_chances` (each monster's swipe and star apart; `take_chances` is their sum), and `sim.world.AIR_PICK` ('area', or 'v83': as from the ground, from where she is). With the defaults nothing changes: all 66 golden traces match.
- Every probe, in a world with reach's answer and in one with the other, has its log decide the world's answer (20 runs in `tests/test_probe.py`, and on each of seeds 1-8: the table under Probes to run; below-42 alone decides the claw off her level on 3 of them, claw-down on all). step-up can't tell the lines apart, and no probe here one-sided walls from walls that stop either way (Probes to run).
- Not measured in game: every answer in that table. An Orange Mushroom 42 below reaches the stabs in its taller frames, which the scorer and the world, going by walking bodies, leave out (below-42 throws at them only past the boxes); in the world as in the scorer, a star's line ends at a walking body, so step-up's corner (a body lifted off its feet right behind the step's edge) is only a few hits a log would show by distance.

**After the review of the three above** (2026-10-20, a correctness review and a fidelity review, each finding checked before it was acted on):
- `decide` (`fight.kill`, `hunt kill`) walked her 0 units: with every spot near enough a target shielded (a monster the attack takes first, its star stopped) it kept them all, "as monsters move", and at the nearest already it walked 0 units, which `Actions.walk` makes a tap, back and forth on the spot until the kill's patience ran out; at her platform's end with a target 410-425 off, the same. Now it keeps shielded spots only 15 units or more away (`stance.MIN_MOVE`) and skips the target if there's none, and throws from where she is when that's the one clear spot.
- The search's bands take a body only past their edges, as the client's `in_step` (strict overlap): a body whose far side was right at a band's near edge was taken in it, a unit closer than the client.
- A probe counts a walled monster in front as one that may take the attack (the client checks the line to its pick alone); it used to leave it out with the wall rule on, and throw at a target behind it as if alone. No probe here has such a wall across its line with the rule on.
- `probe score` decided below-42 on one or two throws that differ (seed 7, both worlds): with every throw the answers agree on a hit, as in the simulator, a single "none" weighed up to 3.9. The hit rates are kept 5% from 0 and 1 (`probe.RATE_FLOOR`, was 2%), so one weighs at most 2.9, and an answer is decided only on 5 or more throws that differ (`probe.DECIDE_THROWS`). The leads fall 0-0.9 (the table under Probes to run); of the 160 runs, three are no longer decided (below-42 seed 7 in both worlds; hanging-wall seed 6 in reach's, on 4 throws), and none decides the other answer.
- Kept, and said so (Attack: her decisions): she throws at a monster behind one the attack takes first whose star isn't stopped. Step 2 had asked that a target count only if it would be the pick; but that one takes the stars until it's dead and then hers comes, where only a walled one, which no star kills, keeps them from it.
- Corrected: the claw off her level (an Orange Mushroom exactly 40 below is in stabO1's box: none is swiped whose feet are 30 or more above hers, nor one more than its height less 15 below them), the search table's snail column (reach's half width, 19: 126, 46, 166, 246), the search 42 below in reach's comments (a pig from 71 and an Orange Mushroom from 33, not both from 90), the logs' lean against the top of 44 (2-5 log-likelihood, past the scorer's bound for independent throws, but in four episodes: all four the same way is p 0.06, suggestive, not decisive), and the case for the level line (meowdb's claim that floors stop a star going down was said to be carried over from Energy Bolt's tests; the page cites no test, and two sources have floors stop it against one, discounted, that has none). The escort's throws from a rope (tour seed 28 with her decisions on the v83 line) went under Found offline.

**After the review of the rebase** (2026-10-21, a merge-fidelity review and a combined-behaviour review, each finding checked before it was acted on; the merge itself held up):
- `probe score` took the positions of the look after the key as the pick's. Now a monster that look saw walking is as likely anywhere it walks from 40 ms before it to 40 ms after it (`probe.placings`), and the log keeps each monster's way and pose for that. The review had it moved back by the looks' times (4 units for a pig); in the simulator that made claw-edge's seed 2 in the old claw's world lean 14.7 the wrong way, not 2.9, since there the look shows its monsters 20 ms before the pick: a look's frame is older than its time, drawn 40 ms before it in the simulator (`fakes.LOOK_LAG`; the key goes down 10 ms after the plan is handed over), and in game a look's time is when its reading ends, after the grab (`eyes.look_at`), by how much isn't measured, nor how far the screen lags the game. With the slack it decides the old box by 5.3, and of the 160 runs of the probes' table 159 are decided and none leans the wrong way (155 and one by 2.9 before).
- claw-edge's runs in `tests/test_probe.py` make 40 throws, as `probe run claw-edge` does: with 20, seed 1 was decided in both worlds on 5 throws that differ, the least that decides; with 40, on 13-14.
- claw-down with the game's errors decided its question on 1 of seeds 1-8 in reach's world and 2 in the swings' (seeds 3 and 5 leaning the stabs' way): she stood 704-720, not 714, and a pig thrown at 88-100 off at the look was within the swings' reach at the pick on 13-37 of 40 throws. It throws by where a pig will be at the pick now (`Probe.at_pick`, within 95), 80 times: both worlds decided on all 8 seeds, with the game's errors or without (Probes to run), and a test runs it with them. Over all 160 runs with the game's errors, 145 are decided, against 129, none the wrong way.
- `probe_top` is back on seed 1, its expect restated: a hit at the pig 111 off or more at the pick, and only there (so on all of seeds 1-16), some under 150. "Every throw hits" held only on seeds 6 and 12 since pigs walk 125 units/s, and on 6 no throw was read as a miss even with hit monsters hidden, so the test of that checked none; on seed 1 it checks 2.
- The tracker's table (Continuous perception: Tracking) is this branch's now: her kills judged right 94.6% of the time over the seven runs, against 94.7% at master's head.
- `test_probe`'s comments went with these; the pre-rebase tip of the branch is tagged `crossval-pre-rebase` (local), as its reports cite it.
- Golden: 70 of the 73 traces as before; `probe_step_up` and `probe_air` differ only by their logs' new fields, `probe_top` by its seed. Nothing else she does changed, so the harness runs above (`cvm-*`) stand.

**Arrows right after an attack** (found in game on 2026-10-21, fixed the same night; see In game, and Control). Every plan's first arrow waits until 0.6 s after her last attack's key (`actions.ARROW_LOCK_MS`, in `Actions.hold_back`), and the simulator's `Realism.lock_arrows` does what the game was seen to do (`world.ARROW_LOCK`, `ARROW_HELD`): an arrow pressed within 0.55 s of the key of an attack the game took is seen 1.0 s after the key if it's still held, and else never (the world's log says which). Before, it kept her still for the whole 730 ms lock whatever was pressed when, and no preset had it; now preset `game`, the two noisy scenarios and `nav_walk_off_after_kill` do.
- Golden: 7 of the 73 traces changed, each first at a walk or a chain that now goes 0.09-0.41 s later, its first arrow 0.6 s after a throw's key (`nav_fight_up`, `nav_fight_across`, `tour_hunt_platform_12`, `loop_spot_off_ground`, `loop_leaves_empty_ground`, `loop_lull_loot`, `loop_rival`); fights and hunts go their own way from there. `loop_lull_loot` moved to seed 2 (on seed 1 no lull came: explained there). The noisy scenarios didn't change: none of their plans pressed an arrow that soon after an attack. `nav_walk_off_after_kill` is new: without the hold-back its walk-off goes 0.34 s after the key and leaves her standing on 3, as in game, and the one sent again a second later lands (`tests/test_sim.py`).
- Harness, preset `game` (now with the arrows swallowed), 10 seeds of 20 minutes, against master (`cvm-*`, which master's head reproduces run for run): the tour 91.5 -> 91.5 EXP a minute (better on 4 of 10 seeds, 4 the same, the worst -12%), drops picked up 92.9% -> 93.4%, HP lost 2.69 -> 2.79 bars; the hunt loop 126.0 -> 123.7 (better on 3, the worst -15%), drops 89.1% -> 89.8%, HP lost 2.82 -> 2.69. In that world without the hold-back she made 92.8 and 127.1, with 20 (tour) and 70 (loop) arrows swallowed and 26 and 22 moves ending elsewhere than planned, against none and 17 and 5 with it: here a move that doesn't go is replanned within about a second, and costs about what the waits do. In game it cost more (In game: 12 interruptions in the 5-minute hunt loop before, 3 in the one after).

**Chains on the night's live misses** (2026-10-21, offline, from that night's telemetry and logs; see In game, Motion planning, Control).
- **A step after a climb.** 25 chains climbed 36 -> 32 and stepped back on 32 for the jump onto 29 before 01:13 (a step of 18-30 units, held 104-170 ms), its arrow pressed before the top of the rope as a run's is. Her dot went 8-30 units past the planned stop in 13 (575 or more, for 557-567), the jump off 32 left that much further on, and 17 of the 25 came down in the basin: 8 short of 29, 9 short of 28 off the jump after it, whose run on 29 started further on too (once 7 in a row). With the step pressed at its planned time (a live patch), the 6 chains after stopped at their spot or short of it and landed every jump. `flow.STEP_EARLY_MAX_MS` (250 ms) keeps a step at its planned time; a run still goes down before the top.
- **Takeoffs with room to spare.** The route checks each jump with 12 units' slop at its own takeoff; chained, the pick up to 10-12 units further back, the kick's wait and a landing before it move the takeoff and use that up, and the player saw jumps from short platforms take off too early. Of the 81 route jumps, chained from standing at their spot, 20 units either side and running in, with rng picks, 47 had takeoffs that missed their platform from 8 units further back or 6% slower (the investigation's check), landing as little as 2.6 units onto it; the jumps 32 -> 29, 29 -> 28 and 16 -> 14 that came down short live were planned 5-16 units onto their platforms. Now a chained jump takes off only where it lands 9 units in from the ends and still lands from 8 units early or late, 10% slower or at 0.85 × 149 (`Flow.sure_takeoff`, `TAKEOFF_SLOP`), else from its next takeoff, and not within 8 units of her platform's end; a hop lands so on her own platform. None of the 81 misses the check now, the least room 9.2 units (`tests/test_flow.py`). The new `nav_climb_then_jump` climbs 36 -> 32 and jumps on to 29 and 28 with the lags spread: before, 28 of 144 trips (seeds 1-48 from x 300, 470 and 760) missed a landing; now none, and with only the takeoffs fixed, 7 of 48 still miss 28.
- **The first arrow after a throw** (In game: Arrows right after an attack). Held back to 0.6 s after the key she still set off 0.2 s late, and the "rope grabs from 16 toward 6 that dropped her onto 25" were jumps from 16 up onto 14 keyed at their planned time after a late start. `actions.ARROW_LOCK_MS` is 0.8 s, and the simulator's `lock_arrows` acts on an arrow pressed less than 0.784 s after an attack's key from 0.835 s after it, if still held (`world.ARROW_LOCK`, `ARROW_HELD`; 0.55 and 1.0 before; 0.72 and 0.77 since the review that followed, timed by when she went off the end: In game). The new `nav_jump_after_kill` shoots a pig on 18 from 16 and goes up to 6 that way: held back to 0.6 s she misses 14 on all 7 seeds of 1-8 that kill the pig, as in game; held back to 0.8 s she lands on all 7.
- **Off the path** (Control: a route): her dot still for 3 glances, not 2, before she counts as standing; nothing counted while the plan has her in the air; standing still where a run starts while the plan has her 30 units on cuts the plan. With the arrows held back to 0.6 s again, that cuts 4 of `nav_jump_after_kill`'s 7 short on 16, which then go on to 6; the other 3 still fall. A trip also waits out a long fall (`FALL_LOOKS`): in `nav_monsters_long_way` on seeds 2 and 4 a touch knocked her down the 12-unit gap beside platform 3 and the trip ended "lost: not on any platform".
- **Bringing the game forward** (Use): the hunting commands wait up to 3 s for the game to come to the front and stop if it won't, where they went on into looks of another window and a refused plan.
- Golden: 17 of the 74 traces changed with the steps and takeoffs (trips without monsters make the same moves in the same time, a jump keyed 9-28 ms later from further on; fights and hunts go their own way), 9 with the arrows (each first at a chain or walk right after a throw, 0.2 s later), none with the rest; `nav_climb_then_jump` and `nav_jump_after_kill` are new. Three scenarios moved seed, explained in their docstrings: `nav_monsters_long_way` 2 -> 5, `nav_fight_across_runs_out` 2 -> 9 (on 2 she now arrives), `loop_lull_loot` 3 -> 4.
- Harness, preset `game`, 10 seeds of 20 minutes, against step 1 (`step30-*`): the tour 88.8 -> 91.9 EXP a minute (better on 5 of 10 seeds, the worst -13%), drops picked up 93.8% -> 90.3% (seed 8 82% -> 48%, seed 9 99% -> 82%; the median 96.3% -> 97.2%), HP lost 2.53 -> 2.66 bars; the hunt loop 123.2 -> 128.4 (better on 7, the worst -16%), drops 85.3% -> 83.4%, HP lost 2.58 -> 2.64. Landings elsewhere with no touch in the 3 s before: tour 14 in 377 chains -> 7 in 384, loop 3 in 214 -> 3 in 175. `compare` fails the bar, as for any change that isn't a better policy.
- **To check in game:** that chains right after a throw set off at once (her dot moving within about 0.25 s of the plan, as with no throw before; none of 16 -> 14 falling to 25); that the step after the climb 36 -> 32 stops at its spot and the jumps on to 29 and 28 land; route jumps' landings (in the telemetry, a chained jump's takeoff and landing well inside its platform: none 2-7 units from its end); that a plan cut "still at x" comes only where she really stood still; and that a hunting command started with another window in front brings the game forward (or says it can't), including whether `SwitchToThisWindow` does it where `SetForegroundWindow` didn't.

**The night's probes, the feed's summary and a sweep's recordings** (2026-10-21, offline, from that night's telemetry and logs; see Probes to run, Continuous perception, Use).
- **Her own platform's monsters in a probe.** claw-edge stood on 21 for 90 s and threw none: a pig or slime of 21 was inside its 95 at 288 of its 533 looks (within 50 at 60), touching her 4 times (1% of her HP), and, of kinds it throws at on its own platform, was never fought however close. step-down fought 2's monsters one at a time as they came within 150, for its whole 150 s, was knocked off onto 4 three times, and threw none. In the simulator, with 2's and 4's monsters and back 8, 15 or 30 s after a kill, fighting them one at a time she threw 1, 0 and 9 times in all over seeds 1-3 (300 s each) and was touched 93-205 times a run. Now she clears her platform first and whenever one of its monsters is in front of her or in her way (`probe.clears_first`): back after 30 s she throws 7, 4 and 5 times, touched 52-79 times; back after 8 or 15 s she threw none either way, and stops after 126-150 s of fighting with no throw (`FIGHT_MAX_S`), saying so, where she fought on to the end. How soon they're back in game isn't measured. Monsters of the kinds it throws at on its own platform (claw-edge's pig and slimes) aren't fought off: on 21 with its three and the snails on 20 and 22, the game's errors and two others allowed in front (300 s, seeds 1 and 3-6), she made 30-40 throws standing, 1-4 fighting them off (knocked into the basin on the way). Standing, though, she threw with them in her way: over 40 throws on seeds 1-8 in reach's world and in the old claw's, 3-11 a run had one of 21's inside 95 in front of her, and those told nothing (the claw's answers differed on 0-5 of them all told, and the other throws alone decided the same: 13 runs right, 1 wrong, 2 undecided). So no probe throws while one of her platform's is in her way, whatever its crowd (`choose`), and she steps away from one she throws at (`GIVE_WAY`, now on): 40 throws took 12-25 minutes (3 of the 16 runs ran out of their 25 at 33-39), deciding 11 right, 1 wrong (the old claw's seed 8, by 5.4) and 4 undecided, with 0.53-0.79 of her HP left (standing, 0.51-0.79). Waiting them out standing instead took 7.5-15 minutes and decided 5 right, 1 wrong, 10 undecided; why the throws it waits for tell less isn't known. Tests: `test_probe.py` (step-down with 2's and 4's monsters back after 30 s and after 8; stepping away on 21, and no throw with one of 21's inside 95 in front of her; no throw while one of her platform's is in her way, whatever the crowd).
- **claw-edge's crowd.** Its 0 throws in 90 s were the snails' more than the slime's: at all 82 looks with a pig or slime of 21 in its range in front of her, a snail on 20 or 22 was in front of her too (both at 81), and a probe threw only with one monster in front. It throws with up to two others in front now (`Probe.crowd`; `--crowd 0` for the lone rule). In the simulator with 20's, 21's and 22's monsters and the game's errors, alone it threw none in 300 s; with two others its 40 throws took 4-6.5 minutes and decided reach's world on 7 of seeds 1-8 (one undecided) and the old claw's on 6 (one undecided, one the other way, seed 5 by 4.8). Neither claw reaches a snail 60 above or below her, so it takes a star the same by either answer; how the one run went the other way isn't known yet. Those runs threw with one of 21's in her way too; now none does (above), and the crowd lets in only others: monsters of other platforms, or of hers past its range. Test: `test_probe.py` (a pig on 21, the snails on 20 and 22: 10 throws in 300 s).
- **The log re-scored by either line** (`probe score --line level`, `--line v83`; the 52 throws of top-60, below-42, other-wall and step-up in `lab/probe_geometry.jsonl`): every question and the window's fits come out the same.
